=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/java_source.py ===
"""Java source declaration metadata using the pinned Tree-sitter Java grammar.
Method bodies/local/anonymous implementation classes are not API declarations.
All parse errors retain byte/line locators into the immutable complete source.
No imported source is executed, compiled, or loaded as a runtime type.
"""
import re,hashlib,importlib.metadata
from tree_sitter import Language,Parser
import tree_sitter_java
from parsers import annotations,typ,sha
LANGUAGE=Language(tree_sitter_java.language())
TYPE_KINDS={'class_declaration':'class','interface_declaration':'interface','enum_declaration':'enum','annotation_type_declaration':'annotation','record_declaration':'class'}
JAVA_LANG={'String','Object','Class','Throwable','Exception','RuntimeException','Error','Enum','Annotation','Deprecated','Override','SuppressWarnings','SafeVarargs','FunctionalInterface','Boolean','Byte','Character','Short','Integer','Long','Float','Double','Void','Number','CharSequence','StringBuilder','StringBuffer','Iterable','Comparable','Cloneable','AutoCloseable'}
def parse(text,source_path='source.java'):
 data=text.encode('utf-8') if isinstance(text,str) else text;root=Parser(LANGUAGE).parse(data).root_node
 records=[];issues=[];package='';imports={};wildcards=[];excluded={};binary_by_owner={}
 def txt(n):return data[n.start_byte:n.end_byte].decode('utf-8',errors='replace') if n else ''
 for n in root.named_children:
  if n.type=='package_declaration':
   ids=[x for x in n.named_children if x.type in ('identifier','scoped_identifier')];package=txt(ids[-1]) if ids else ''
  elif n.type=='import_declaration':
   value=re.sub(r'^import\s+(?:static\s+)?|;\s*$','',txt(n)).strip()
   if value.endswith('.*'):wildcards.append(value[:-2])
   else:imports[value.split('.')[-1]]=value
 def mask_comments(value):
  return re.sub(r'"(?:\\.|[^"\\])*"|\'(?:\\.|[^\'\\])*\'|/\*.*?\*/|//[^\n]*',lambda m:m.group(0) if m.group(0).startswith(('"',"'")) else ''.join('\n' if c=='\n' else ' ' for c in m.group(0)),value,flags=re.S)
 def qualify_anns(value):
  cleaned=mask_comments(value)
  _,items=annotations(cleaned)
  for a in items:
   raw=a['name'];first,sep,tail=raw.partition('.')
   if first in imports:a['name']=imports[first]+(sep+tail if sep else '');a['name_resolution']='explicit-import'
   elif '.' in raw:a['name_resolution']='qualified-source-spelling'
   elif raw in JAVA_LANG:a['name']='java.lang.'+raw;a['name_resolution']='java-lang'
   elif 'android.annotation' in wildcards and raw in ('RequiresPermission','EnforcePermission','RequiresNoPermission','SystemApi','TestApi','FlaggedApi','Nullable','NonNull'):
    a['name']='android.annotation.'+raw;a['name_resolution']='known-annotation-wildcard'
   else:a['name']=(package+'.' if package else '')+raw;a['name_resolution']='package-candidate; unresolved wildcard imports retained'
   a['source_spelling']=raw
   a['source_raw']=value[a['start']:a['end']]
   args=a['arguments']
   for simple,full in imports.items():args=re.sub(r'(?<![\w.$])'+re.escape(simple)+r'(?=\.)',full,args)
   a['source_arguments']=a['arguments'];a['arguments']=args
  return items
 def modifiers(n):
  m=next((c for c in n.named_children if c.type=='modifiers'),None);value=txt(m);clean,_=annotations(mask_comments(value))
  return re.findall(r'\b(public|protected|private|static|final|abstract|native|synchronized|strictfp|default|transient|volatile|sealed|non-sealed)\b',clean),qualify_anns(value)
 def type_text(n,extra=''):
  raw=txt(n)+extra;clean=typ(raw)
  # Resolve explicit imports without guessing wildcard imports/type variables.
  for simple,full in imports.items():
   if full.startswith('java.lang.'):continue
   clean=re.sub(r'(?<![\w.$])'+re.escape(simple)+r'(?![\w$])',full,clean)
  return clean
 def parameter(n):
  t=n.child_by_field_name('type');name=n.child_by_field_name('name');mod,anns=modifiers(n)
  if n.type=='spread_parameter':
   t=t or next((c for c in n.named_children if c.type not in ('modifiers','variable_declarator','annotation','marker_annotation')),None)
   decl=next((c for c in n.named_children if c.type=='variable_declarator'),None);name=name or (decl.child_by_field_name('name') if decl else None)
  dims=''.join(txt(c) for c in n.named_children if c.type=='dimensions')
  return dict(type=type_text(t,('...' if n.type=='spread_parameter' else '')+dims),name=txt(name) or None,direction=None,annotations=qualify_anns(txt(n)),source_type=txt(t),source_byte_range=[n.start_byte,n.end_byte])
 def row(n,kind,owner,name,return_type=None,parameters=None,canonical=None,anns=None,extra=None,raw=None):
  mods,ma=modifiers(n);parameters=parameters or [];body=n.child_by_field_name('body')
  header=data[n.start_byte:body.start_byte].decode('utf8',errors='replace').strip() if body else txt(n).strip()
  if raw is not None:header=raw
  visibility=next((x for x in mods if x in ('public','protected','private')),None)
  defaults=extra or {}
  if visibility is None:visibility='public' if defaults.get('owner_kind') in ('interface','annotation') and kind in ('method','field') else 'package'
  e=dict(source_path=source_path,source_byte_range=[n.start_byte,n.end_byte],modifiers=mods,effective_visibility=visibility,owner_binary_name=binary_by_owner.get(owner),public_sdk_membership='unknown until checked against published SDK metadata');e.update(defaults)
  return dict(kind=kind,canonical=canonical or owner+'#'+name,owner=owner,package=package,name=name,return_type=return_type,parameters=parameters,annotations=ma if anns is None else anns,all_annotations=qualify_anns(header),raw=header,line=n.start_point[0]+1,extras=e)
 def excluded_node(kind):excluded[kind]=excluded.get(kind,0)+1
 def type_decl(n,outer=None,depth=0):
  if depth>128:issues.append(dict(reason='Nested type depth exceeds128',line=n.start_point[0]+1,byte_range=[n.start_byte,n.end_byte]));return
  name=txt(n.child_by_field_name('name'));owner=outer+'.'+name if outer else (package+'.' if package else '')+name;kind=TYPE_KINDS[n.type]
  binary_by_owner[owner]=binary_by_owner[outer]+'$'+name if outer else (package.replace('.','/')+'/' if package else '')+name
  r=row(n,kind,owner,name,canonical=owner,extra=dict(declaration_kind=n.type,type_parameters=txt(n.child_by_field_name('type_parameters')),superclass=txt(n.child_by_field_name('superclass')),interfaces=txt(n.child_by_field_name('interfaces'))));records.append(r)
  body=n.child_by_field_name('body')
  if body is None:return
  def child(c):
   if c.type in TYPE_KINDS:type_decl(c,owner,depth+1)
   elif c.type=='enum_body_declarations':
    for x in c.named_children:child(x)
   elif c.type in ('method_declaration','constructor_declaration','compact_constructor_declaration','annotation_type_element_declaration'):
    ctor=c.type in ('constructor_declaration','compact_constructor_declaration');name=txt(c.child_by_field_name('name'));ps=c.child_by_field_name('parameters');params=[];receivers=[]
    if ps:
     for p in ps.named_children:
      if p.type in ('formal_parameter','spread_parameter'):params.append(parameter(p))
      elif p.type=='receiver_parameter':receivers.append(txt(p))
    canonical=owner+'#'+('<init>' if ctor else name)+'('+','.join(p['type'] for p in params)+')'
    mods,anns=modifiers(c);r=row(c,'constructor' if ctor else 'method',owner,name,None if ctor else type_text(c.child_by_field_name('type')),params,canonical,anns,dict(owner_kind=kind,type_parameters=txt(c.child_by_field_name('type_parameters')),receiver_parameters=receivers,default_value_raw=txt(c.child_by_field_name('value')),source_declared_constructor=ctor))
    records.append(r)
   elif c.type in ('field_declaration','constant_declaration'):
    t=c.child_by_field_name('type');mods,anns=modifiers(c)
    for v in c.named_children:
     if v.type!='variable_declarator':continue
     name=txt(v.child_by_field_name('name'));value=v.child_by_field_name('value');dims=''.join(txt(x) for x in v.named_children if x.type=='dimensions');value_raw=txt(value)
     raw=txt(c);extra=dict(owner_kind=kind,constant_raw=value_raw if len(value_raw)<=4096 else None,initializer_source_range=[value.start_byte,value.end_byte] if value else None,enum_constant=False)
     if len(raw)>16384:raw=type_text(t)+' '+name+' [initializer retained at source byte range]';extra['raw_signature_is_excerpt']=True
     records.append(row(c,'field',owner,name,type_text(t,dims),anns=anns,extra=extra,raw=raw))
   elif c.type=='enum_constant':
    name=txt(c.child_by_field_name('name'));records.append(row(c,'field',owner,name,owner,extra=dict(owner_kind='enum',enum_constant=True,effective_visibility='public')))
   elif c.type not in ('line_comment','block_comment'):excluded_node(c.type)
  for c in body.named_children:child(c)
 if package:
  n=next(n for n in root.named_children if n.type=='package_declaration');records.append(row(n,'package',None,package,canonical=package,extra=dict(declaration_kind='package')))
 for n in root.named_children:
  if n.type in TYPE_KINDS:type_decl(n)
  elif n.type not in ('package_declaration','import_declaration','line_comment','block_comment'):excluded_node(n.type)
 def errors(n):
  if n.type=='ERROR' or n.is_missing:
   issues.append(dict(reason='Java AST '+('missing token' if n.is_missing else 'ERROR'),line=n.start_point[0]+1,byte_range=[n.start_byte,n.end_byte],raw_excerpt=txt(n)[:4096],source_bytes_retained=True))
  elif n.has_error:
   for c in n.children:errors(c)
 errors(root)
 for issue in issues:
  locator=str(issue['byte_range']);raw=issue.get('raw_excerpt','')
  records.append(dict(kind='unsupported',canonical='unparsed-java:'+source_path+':'+locator+':'+sha(raw),owner=None,package=package,name='unparsed',return_type=None,parameters=[],annotations=[],all_annotations=[],raw=raw,line=issue['line'],extras=dict(parse_error=issue['reason'],source_byte_range=issue['byte_range'])))
 return dict(records=records,issues=issues,parse_errors=len(issues),package=package,imports=imports,wildcard_imports=wildcards,excluded_implementation_nodes=excluded,parser='tree-sitter-java'+importlib.metadata.version('tree-sitter-java')+' / tree-sitter'+importlib.metadata.version('tree-sitter'))

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/jvm_source_link.py ===
"""Exact link only when a source type has a proven current SDK JVM spelling.
No wildcard-import guesses or type-variable bound erasure are performed.
Generics' outer types may be erased only when that outer type is known.
"""
PRIMITIVES={'byte':'B','char':'C','double':'D','float':'F','int':'I','long':'J','short':'S','boolean':'Z','void':'V'}
def encode(value,owner,owners):
 if not value:return None
 value=value.replace('...','[]').strip();dims=0
 while value.endswith('[]'):dims+=1;value=value[:-2].strip()
 if value in PRIMITIVES:
  if value=='void' and dims:return None
  return '['*dims+PRIMITIVES[value]
 # Strip balanced parameterized arguments, retaining the outer known type.
 depth=0;outer=[]
 for c in value:
  if c=='<':depth+=1
  elif c=='>':
   depth-=1
   if depth<0:return None
  elif depth==0:outer.append(c)
 if depth:return None
 value=''.join(outer).strip()
 candidate=owners.get(value)
 # Same-package/nested candidates are not assumed in the presence of unresolved
 # imports/type-variable shadowing; only explicitly qualified names link here.
 return '['*dims+'L'+candidate+';' if candidate else None
def key(r,owners):
 owner=r['extras'].get('owner_binary_name')
 if not owner or owners.get(r['owner'])!=owner:return None
 if r['kind'] in ('class','interface','enum','annotation'):return owner
 # The lifecycle index lacks a field descriptor/type. A same-name field is
 # retained as source evidence but cannot prove a typed JVM identity.
 if r['kind']=='field':return None
 if r['kind'] not in ('method','constructor'):return None
 args=[encode(p['type'],r['owner'],owners) for p in r['parameters']]
 ret='V' if r['kind']=='constructor' else encode(r.get('return_type'),r['owner'],owners)
 if ret is None or any(a is None or a=='V' for a in args):return None
 return owner+'#'+('<init>' if r['kind']=='constructor' else r['name'])+'('+''.join(args)+')'+ret

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/migrate_v3.py ===
"""Checked offline COPY migration. Never modifies original release or active files."""
import pathlib,sqlite3,json,os,sys
P=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

def migrate(source,destination):
    source=pathlib.Path(source).resolve();target=pathlib.Path(destination).resolve()
    staging=target.with_suffix(target.suffix+'.staging')
    if target.exists() or staging.exists():raise ValueError('Destination must be new')
    old=sqlite3.connect(source.as_uri()+'?mode=ro',uri=True);old.row_factory=sqlite3.Row
    if old.execute('PRAGMA user_version').fetchone()[0]!=2:raise ValueError('Only storage user_version=2 can migrate to v3')
    if old.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]!='ok' or old.execute('PRAGMA foreign_key_check').fetchall():raise ValueError('Source integrity/FK failure')
    new=sqlite3.connect(staging);new.execute('PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON')
    try:
        for p in sorted((P/'migrations').glob('*.sql')):new.executescript(p.read_text())
        # SQLite optimizer statistics are internal and are regenerated, not imported.
        tables=[r[0] for r in old.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'")]
        new.execute('PRAGMA defer_foreign_keys=ON');new.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        for table in tables:
            if table=='catalog_view':continue
            if table=='effective_entries':
                active=old.execute('SELECT registry_id FROM active_registry WHERE singleton=1').fetchone()
                if not active and old.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM effective_entries').fetchone()[0]:raise ValueError('No active registry for legacy effective entries')
                for row in old.execute('SELECT entry_id,document_json,record_sha256 FROM effective_entries'):
                    new.execute('INSERT INTO registry_entries VALUES(?,?,?,?)',(active[0],row[0],row[1],row[2]))
                if new.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM registry_entries').fetchone()[0]!=old.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM effective_entries').fetchone()[0]:raise ValueError('Entry count mismatch')
                continue
            for row in old.execute('SELECT * FROM "'+table+'"'):
                r=dict(row)
                if table in ('registries','import_jobs') and 'status' in r:r['state']=r.pop('status')
                if table=='permission_relations':r['logic']=json.loads(r['expression_json'])['op']
                if table=='catalog_meta' and r['key']=='schema_version':r['value']='1.2.0'
                if table=='effective_entries':r['etag']='"cat1:1:'+r['record_sha256']+':'+r['entry_id']+':0:active"'
                destination_table='legacy_import_jobs' if table=='import_jobs' else table
                cols=','.join('"'+k+'"' for k in r)
                new.execute('INSERT INTO "'+destination_table+'"('+cols+') VALUES('+','.join('?' for _ in r)+')',list(r.values()))
            actual=new.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM "'+('legacy_import_jobs' if table=='import_jobs' else table)+'"').fetchone()[0]
            expected=old.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM "'+table+'"').fetchone()[0]
            if actual!=expected:raise ValueError('Count mismatch '+table)
        new.execute("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO catalog_meta VALUES('rebuilt_with_corrected_extractor','false')")
        new.execute('UPDATE catalog_view SET registry_generation=(SELECT registry_version FROM registries WHERE registry_id=(SELECT registry_id FROM active_registry WHERE singleton=1)) WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM active_registry)')
        if new.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]!='ok' or new.execute('PRAGMA foreign_key_check').fetchall():raise ValueError('Migrated integrity/FK failure')
        new.commit();new.close();old.close();os.rename(staging,target)
    except Exception:
        new.rollback();new.close();old.close()
        # Preserve failed staging bytes for diagnosis; never present as published output.
        raise

if __name__=='__main__':migrate(sys.argv[1],sys.argv[2])

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/parsers.py ===
"""Conservative source-declaration parsers. No runtime access or wire-codec claims."""
import re,json,hashlib
from foundation_extract import split_top,annotations as old_annotations,permission_ast as old_ast

def sha(x):return hashlib.sha256(x.encode()).hexdigest()
def annotations(text):
 # Equal-length masking preserves source offsets. Quoted @ signs are ordinary data.
 chars=list(text);anns=[];i=0;quote=None;esc=False
 while i<len(text):
  c=text[i]
  if quote:
   if esc:esc=False
   elif c=='\\':esc=True
   elif c==quote:quote=None
   i+=1;continue
  if c in ('"',"'"):quote=c;i+=1;continue
  if c!='@':i+=1;continue
  m=re.match(r'@([\w.$]+)',text[i:])
  if not m:i+=1;continue
  name=m.group(1)
  if name=='interface':i+=len(m.group(0));continue
  a=i;b=i+len(m.group(0));end=b;args='';j=b
  while j<len(text) and text[j].isspace():j+=1
  if j<len(text) and text[j]=='(':
   depth=0;q=None;escape=False;end=None
   for k in range(j,len(text)):
    cc=text[k]
    if q:
     if escape:escape=False
     elif cc=='\\':escape=True
     elif cc==q:q=None
    elif cc in ('"',"'"):q=cc
    elif cc=='(':depth+=1
    elif cc==')':
     depth-=1
     if depth==0:end=k+1;args=text[j+1:k];break
   if end is None:raise ValueError('Unclosed annotation')
  anns.append(dict(name=name,arguments=args,raw=text[a:end],start=a,end=end))
  chars[a:end]=['\n' if x=='\n' else ' ' for x in text[a:end]];i=end
 return ''.join(chars),anns
def unknown(reason,raw=None):
 d=dict(op='unknown',reason=reason)
 if raw is not None:d['raw']=raw
 return d
def permission(anns,symbols=None,aidl=False):
 symbols=symbols or {};clauses=[]
 def resolve(a):
  if a.get('op')=='unknown' and a.get('reason')=='Unresolved symbolic permission expression' and a.get('raw') in symbols:
   return dict(op='permission',name=symbols[a['raw']],**{'x-symbol':a['raw']})
  if a.get('op')=='permission':
   name=a['name']
   if name in symbols:return dict(op='permission',name=symbols[name],**{'x-symbol':name})
   if name.startswith('Manifest.permission.') or '.Manifest.permission.' in name or '.Permission.' in name or re.search(r'\s',name):return unknown('Source annotation contains a symbolic reference or non-permission predicate, not a proven literal permission name',name)
   if aidl and '.' not in name:return unknown('AIDL short permission namespace not resolved by this extractor',name)
  return {k:resolve(v) if isinstance(v,dict) else [resolve(t) if isinstance(t,dict) else t for t in v] if isinstance(v,list) else v for k,v in a.items()}
 for a in anns:
  n=a['name'];short=n.split('.')[-1]
  if short not in ('RequiresPermission','EnforcePermission','PermissionManuallyEnforced','RequiresNoPermission','Read','Write'):continue
  if short in ('Read','Write') and 'RequiresPermission' not in n:continue
  args=a['arguments']
  # AIDL accepts array syntax in addition to Java's brace syntax.
  if aidl:args=re.sub(r'(allOf|anyOf)\s*=\s*\[([^\]]*)\]',r'\1={\2}',args)
  clauses.append(resolve(old_ast(n,args)))
 return clauses[0] if len(clauses)==1 else dict(op='all',children=clauses) if clauses else unknown('No declared recognized permission metadata; absence is not permission-free')

def typ(text):
 clean,_=annotations(text);clean=re.sub(r'\b(final|optional)\s+','',clean).strip()
 clean=re.sub(r'\s+',' ',clean);clean=re.sub(r'\s*([<>,\[\]])\s*',r'\1',clean)
 return clean
def param(text,aidl=False):
 clean,anns=annotations(text);clean=clean.strip();direction=None;name=None
 if aidl:
  m=re.match(r'(inout|in|out)\s+',clean)
  if m:direction=m.group(1);clean=clean[m.end():]
  m=re.fullmatch(r'(.+?)\s+([\w$]+)',clean,re.S)
  if not m:raise ValueError('AIDL parameter type/name unresolved')
  clean,name=m.groups()
 else:
  clean=re.sub(r'\b(final|optional)\s+','',clean)
  m=re.fullmatch(r'(.+?)\s+([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)(?:\s*=.*)?',clean,re.S)
  if m and not m.group(1).endswith(('extends','super')):clean,name=m.groups()
 return dict(type=typ(clean),name=name,direction=direction,annotations=anns)
def head_return(head):
 h=re.sub(r'^(?:(?:public|protected|private|static|final|abstract|default|synchronized|native|strictfp|deprecated|inline|operator|suspend|oneway)\s+)+','',head).strip()
 if h.startswith('<'):
  depth=0
  for i,c in enumerate(h):
   if c=='<':depth+=1
   elif c=='>':
    depth-=1
    if depth==0:h=h[i+1:].strip();break
 return typ(h)

def member(raw,owner,package,aidl=False):
 clean_full,allanns=annotations(raw);clean=clean_full.strip().rstrip(';').strip()
 kind='aidl-member' if aidl else clean.split()[0]
 body=clean if aidl else clean[len(kind):].strip()
 if '(' in body and (aidl or kind in ('ctor','method')):
  a=body.index('(');b=body.rfind(')')
  if b<a:raise ValueError('Unclosed parameter list')
  h=body[:a].strip();m=re.search(r'([\w$]+)$',h)
  if not m:raise ValueError('Unresolved callable name')
  name=m.group(1);ret=None if kind=='ctor' else head_return(h[:m.start()].strip())
  original_a=clean_full.index('(');original_b=clean_full.rfind(')')
  params=[param(x,aidl) for x in split_top(raw[original_a+1:original_b]) if x.strip()]
  # Capture declaration annotations separately; parameter annotations cannot grant the callable authority.
  anns=[a for a in allanns if a['end']<=original_a]
  canonical=owner+'#'+('<init>' if kind=='ctor' else name)+'('+','.join(p['type'] for p in params)+')'
  extra={}
  if aidl:
   suffix=body[b+1:].strip();explicit=re.fullmatch(r'=\s*(\d+)',suffix)
   extra=dict(oneway=bool(re.search(r'\boneway\b',body[:a])),declared_method_id=int(explicit.group(1)) if explicit else None,wire_transaction_code=None,wire_status='unknown; generated Binder transaction constants and codecs not imported')
   if suffix and not explicit:extra['unresolved_suffix']=suffix
  return dict(kind='constructor' if kind=='ctor' else 'method' if kind=='method' else kind,canonical=canonical,owner=owner,package=package,name=name,return_type=ret,parameters=params,annotations=anns,all_annotations=allanns,raw=raw,extras=extra)
 if aidl:raise ValueError('AIDL non-method statement preserved but not interpreted')
 if kind not in ('field','enum_constant','property'):raise ValueError('Unsupported signature member')
 h=body.split('=',1)[0].strip();m=re.search(r'([\w$]+)$',h)
 if not m:raise ValueError('Unresolved field name')
 name=m.group(1);value=body.split('=',1)[1].strip() if '=' in body else None
 return dict(kind='field' if kind=='enum_constant' else kind,canonical=owner+'#'+name,owner=owner,package=package,name=name,return_type=head_return(h[:m.start()].strip()),parameters=[],annotations=allanns,all_annotations=allanns,raw=raw,extras=dict(constant_raw=value,enum_constant=kind=='enum_constant'))

def signature(text):
 package=None;owner=None;scopes=[]
 for line,raw in enumerate(text.splitlines(),1):
  s=raw.strip()
  if not s or s.startswith('//'):continue
  if s=='}':
   if scopes:package,owner=scopes.pop()
   else:package=None;owner=None
   continue
  if s.endswith('{'):scopes.append((package,owner))
  try:
   clean,anns=annotations(s)
   if clean.startswith('package '):
    package=clean.split()[1];owner=None;r=dict(kind='package',canonical=package,package=package,owner=None,name=package,parameters=[],return_type=None,annotations=anns,all_annotations=anns,raw=s,extras={})
   else:
    m=re.search(r'(?<![\w.])(@interface|class|interface|enum)\s+([\w.$]+)',clean)
    if m and clean.endswith('{'):
     if not package:raise ValueError('Type without package')
     kind='annotation' if m.group(1)=='@interface' else m.group(1);name=m.group(2);owner=package+'.'+name;r=dict(kind=kind,canonical=owner,package=package,owner=owner,name=name,parameters=[],return_type=None,annotations=anns,all_annotations=anns,raw=s,extras=dict(type_header=clean))
    else:
     if not owner:raise ValueError('Member without owner')
     r=member(s,owner,package)
   r['line']=line;yield r
  except Exception as e:
   if s.endswith('{'):owner=None
   yield dict(kind='unsupported',canonical='unparsed:'+sha(s),package=package or '',owner=owner,name='unparsed',parameters=[],return_type=None,annotations=[],all_annotations=[],raw=s,line=line,extras=dict(parse_error=str(e)))

def without_comments(text):
 # Keep string/char literals intact, including // and /* inside quoted strings.
 return re.sub(r'"(?:\\.|[^"\\])*"|\'(?:\\.|[^\'\\])*\'|/\*.*?\*/|//[^\n]*',lambda m:m.group(0) if m.group(0).startswith(('"',"'")) else '\n'*m.group(0).count('\n')+' ',text,flags=re.S)

def aidl(text):
 code=without_comments(text);p=re.search(r'\bpackage\s+([\w.]+)\s*;',code);pkg=p.group(1) if p else ''
 imports=re.findall(r'\bimport\s+([\w.]+)\s*;',code)
 clean,anns=annotations(code)
 d=re.search(r'\b(interface|parcelable|union|enum)\s+([\w]+)',clean)
 if not d:return dict(package=pkg,descriptor=None,declaration_kind=None,imports=imports,annotations=anns,members=[],parse_status='unsupported',reason='No supported top-level AIDL declaration')
 kind,name=d.groups();descriptor=(pkg+'.' if pkg else '')+name
 # Locate the actual declaration before stripping annotations, preserving raw member annotation arguments.
 dm=re.search(r'\b'+kind+r'\s+'+re.escape(name)+r'\b',code)
 prefix=code[:dm.start()];_,topanns=annotations(prefix[prefix.rfind(';')+1:])
 start=code.find('{',dm.end());members=[];status='declaration-only'
 if start>=0:
  depth=0;quote=None;esc=False;end=None
  for i,c in enumerate(code[start:],start):
   if quote:
    if esc:esc=False
    elif c=='\\':esc=True
    elif c==quote:quote=None
   elif c in ('"',"'"):quote=c
   elif c=='{':depth+=1
   elif c=='}':
    depth-=1
    if depth==0:end=i;break
  if end is None:return dict(package=pkg,descriptor=descriptor,declaration_kind=kind,imports=imports,annotations=topanns,members=[],parse_status='unsupported',reason='Unbalanced declaration body')
  body=code[start+1:end]
  if kind=='interface':
   chunks=[c.strip() for c in split_top(body,';') if c.strip()];status='methods-parsed'
   offset=0
   for ordinal,raw in enumerate(chunks):
    locator=body.find(raw,offset);offset=locator+len(raw)
    try:r=member(raw,descriptor,pkg,True);r['extras']['declaration_ordinal']=ordinal
    except Exception as e:r=dict(kind='unsupported',canonical=descriptor+'#unparsed:'+sha(raw),owner=descriptor,package=pkg,name='unparsed',return_type=None,parameters=[],annotations=annotations(raw)[1],all_annotations=annotations(raw)[1],raw=raw,extras=dict(parse_error=str(e)));status='partial'
    r['line']=code[:start+1].count('\n')+1+body[:locator].count('\n');members.append(r)
  else:status='structured-body-retained; fields/enumerants/union tags unresolved'
 return dict(package=pkg,descriptor=descriptor,declaration_kind=kind,imports=imports,annotations=topanns,members=members,parse_status=status,reason='All source bytes retained; generated transaction IDs, resolved imports and wire codecs unknown',oneway=bool(re.search(r'\boneway\s+interface\s+'+re.escape(name),code)))

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/policy_reference.py ===
"""Offline decision algebra. Not authentication, an Android backend or an MCP service."""
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Rule:
    effect: str
    schema_hash: str
    allowed_targets: frozenset
    max_items: int

def resolve_fixture_route(route,request,aliases,schemas,wire_mappings):
    """Fixture-only canonicalization, with explicitly supplied proven wire mappings.

    No Binder transaction codes or decoders are inferred from AIDL declaration order.
    The caller supplies a decoded canonical fixture argument; a real backend must
    prove that its byte decoder produces this same typed representation.
    """
    if route=='generated':
        binding,schema=aliases[request['tool_name']]
    elif route=='generic':
        binding=request['binding_id'];schema=schemas[binding]
        if request['schema_hash']!=schema:raise ValueError('Stale generic schema')
    elif route=='raw':
        key=(request['descriptor'],request['transaction_code'])
        if key not in wire_mappings:raise ValueError('Unknown raw requires separate broad service authority')
        if not request.get('decoded_and_validated'):raise ValueError('Known raw cannot bypass canonical argument validation')
        binding,schema=wire_mappings[key]
    else:raise ValueError('Unknown route')
    if schemas.get(binding)!=schema:raise ValueError('Stale alias or wire mapping')
    return [(binding,schema,request['target'],request['items'])]

def evaluate(binding_plan, binding_rules, entry_rule, *, entry_schema, authority, emergency=False):
    """All aliases supply the SAME decoded binding plan; raw unknown uses a raw key.

    Target and numeric bounds here are fixture examples of the closed constraint
    schemas. Real adapters must validate every argument/output field before admission.
    Each tuple is (canonical binding key, exact schema hash, target, requested items).
    """
    if emergency or not binding_plan or entry_rule is None or entry_rule.schema_hash!=entry_schema: return 'deny'
    effects=[]
    for key, schema, target, items in binding_plan:
        rule=binding_rules.get(key)
        if rule is None or rule.schema_hash!=schema: return 'deny'
        for r in (rule,entry_rule):
            if r.effect not in ('allow','ask') or target not in r.allowed_targets or not isinstance(items,int) or isinstance(items,bool) or items<0 or items>r.max_items: return 'deny'
            effects.append(r.effect)
    if not authority: return 'os-unavailable'
    return 'ask' if 'ask' in effects else 'admit-candidate'

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RawGrant:
    connection_id: str
    descriptor: str
    code_min: int
    code_max: int
    flags_mask: int
    contract_hash: str
    rule: Rule
    allowed_handles: frozenset = frozenset()
    allowed_displays: frozenset = frozenset()

def evaluate_raw_fixture(request,raw_grants,entry_rule,*,entry_schema,authority,wire_mappings,schemas,binding_rules,emergency=False,binding_entry_rule=None,binding_entry_schema='binding.invoke-v1'):
    """Known schemas always take binding policy; raw grants cannot override it."""
    if (request['descriptor'],request['transaction_code']) in wire_mappings:
        try:plan=resolve_fixture_route('raw',request,{},schemas,wire_mappings)
        except (KeyError,ValueError):return 'deny'
        checks=[evaluate(plan,binding_rules,binding_entry_rule,entry_schema=binding_entry_schema,authority=True,emergency=emergency),evaluate(plan,binding_rules,entry_rule,entry_schema=entry_schema,authority=True,emergency=emergency)]
        if 'deny' in checks:return 'deny'
        if not authority:return 'os-unavailable'
        return 'ask' if 'ask' in checks else 'admit-candidate'
    key=(request.get('connection_id'),request['descriptor']);grant=raw_grants.get(key)
    if grant is None or grant.connection_id!=key[0] or grant.descriptor!=key[1] or grant.contract_hash!=request.get('contract_hash'):return 'deny'
    if request.get('handle') not in grant.allowed_handles or request.get('display_id') not in grant.allowed_displays:return 'deny'
    code=request['transaction_code'];flags=request.get('flags',0)
    if not isinstance(code,int) or isinstance(code,bool) or not grant.code_min<=code<=grant.code_max:return 'deny'
    if not isinstance(flags,int) or isinstance(flags,bool) or flags<0 or flags&~grant.flags_mask:return 'deny'
    raw_key='raw:'+grant.connection_id+':'+grant.descriptor
    return evaluate([(raw_key,grant.rule.schema_hash,request['target'],request['items'])],{raw_key:grant.rule},entry_rule,entry_schema=entry_schema,authority=authority,emergency=emergency)

def policy_entrypoints(route,known_raw=False):
    if route in ('generated','generic'):return ('binding.invoke',)
    if route=='raw':return ('binding.invoke','binder.transact') if known_raw else ('binder.transact',)
    raise ValueError('Convenience wrappers use their own registered policy tool_id')

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/restore_snapshot.py ===
"""Restore exact frozen source bytes, including SDK ZIP metadata, on explicit request.
Dry run by default. Stops on access denial; temporary429 uses the same URL with
Retry-After backoff. Does not install SDKs, accept licenses, or read platform
binary members. Existing mismatching bytes cause failure, never silent overwrite.
"""
import pathlib,json,hashlib,urllib.request,urllib.error,time,datetime,email.utils,zipfile,xml.etree.ElementTree as ET,sys
from sdk_zip_metadata import extract_metadata
def get(url,headers=None):
 for retry in range(3):
  time.sleep(1)
  try:return urllib.request.urlopen(urllib.request.Request(url,headers=headers or {}),timeout=60)
  except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
   if e.code!=429 or retry==2:raise
   pause=60*(retry+1);header=e.headers.get('Retry-After')
   if header:
    try:pause=max(pause,float(header))
    except ValueError:pause=max(pause,(email.utils.parsedate_to_datetime(header)-datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc)).total_seconds())
   while pause>0:step=min(pause,60);time.sleep(step);pause-=step
def verify(data,s):
 if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()!=s['sha256']:raise ValueError('Source SHA256 mismatch '+s['path'])
 if hashlib.sha1(b'blob '+str(len(data)).encode()+b'\0'+data).hexdigest()!=s['git_blob']:raise ValueError('Source local address mismatch '+s['path'])
def restore_member(s,root):
 t=s['transport'];archive=root/t['archive_local_path']
 if not archive.exists():
  archive.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);temporary=archive.with_suffix('.download-partial')
  with get(t['archive_url']) as response,temporary.open('wb') as f:
   count=0
   while b:=response.read(1024*1024):
    count+=len(b)
    if count>64*1024*1024:raise ValueError('Source-data archive exceeds bounded size')
    f.write(b)
  data=temporary.read_bytes()
  if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()!=t['archive_sha256'] or hashlib.sha1(data).hexdigest()!=t['archive_sha1']:raise ValueError('Source archive hash mismatch')
  temporary.replace(archive)
 data=archive.read_bytes()
 if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()!=t['archive_sha256'] or hashlib.sha1(data).hexdigest()!=t['archive_sha1']:raise ValueError('Existing source archive hash mismatch')
 with zipfile.ZipFile(archive) as z:return z.read(t['archive_member'])
def restore_metadata(s,root):
 t=s['transport'];receipt=json.loads((root/t['metadata_receipt']).read_text());url=t['archive_url'];tag=receipt['etag']
 metadata=ET.parse(root/'repository2-3.xml').getroot();package=next(p for p in metadata.findall('remotePackage') if p.attrib['path']=='platforms;android-37.2');size=int(package.findtext('archives/archive/complete/size'))
 def part(start,count):
  with get(url,{'Range':f'bytes={start}-{start+count-1}','If-Match':tag,'Accept-Encoding':'identity'}) as response:
   if response.status!=206 or response.headers.get('Content-Range')!=f'bytes {start}-{start+count-1}/{size}' or response.headers.get('ETag')!=tag:raise ValueError('Metadata range/version mismatch; refusing full platform download')
   data=response.read(count+1)
   if len(data)!=count:raise ValueError('Metadata range length mismatch')
   return data
 return extract_metadata(size,part)[0]
def main(inventory,download=False):
 inv=pathlib.Path(inventory).resolve();root=inv.parent;sources=json.loads(inv.read_text())['sources'];missing=[];seen=set()
 for s in sources:
  if s['status']!='downloaded' or s['git_blob'] in seen:continue
  seen.add(s['git_blob']);p=root/s['local_path']
  if p.exists():verify(p.read_bytes(),s)
  else:missing.append(s)
 print('Missing exact source blobs:',len(missing),flush=True)
 if not download:return
 metadata_cache={};source_archives={}
 import base64
 for s in missing:
  t=s.get('transport',{});kind=t.get('kind')
  if kind=='sdk-zip-member':
   # Cache selected archive members in memory once, bounded by source archive.
   key=t['archive_url']
   if key not in source_archives:
    first=restore_member(s,root);archive=root/t['archive_local_path']
    source_archives[key]=zipfile.ZipFile(archive)
   data=source_archives[key].read(t['archive_member'])
  elif kind=='bounded-sdk-zip-range-member':
   key=t['archive_url']
   if key not in metadata_cache:metadata_cache[key]=restore_metadata(s,root)
   data=metadata_cache[key][t['archive_member']]
  else:
   with get(s['url']+'?format=TEXT') as response:data=base64.b64decode(response.read(),validate=True)
  verify(data,s);p=root/s['local_path'];p.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);tmp=p.with_suffix('.restoring');tmp.write_bytes(data);tmp.replace(p)
 for archive in source_archives.values():archive.close()
if __name__=='__main__':main(sys.argv[1],'--download' in sys.argv)

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/sdk_api_index.py ===
"""Published SDK API-version index: exact JVM member keys, conservative semantics.
The index is not a Java signature file: type kind, field types, modifiers,
constants, generics, throws and permission annotations are not encoded here.
Historical/removed and future declarations are enumerated separately.
"""
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET,xml.parsers.expat,collections
PRIMITIVES={'B':'byte','C':'char','D':'double','F':'float','I':'int','J':'long','S':'short','Z':'boolean','V':'void'}
def version(v):
 try:return tuple(map(int,v.split('.')))+(0,)*(2-len(v.split('.')))
 except (AttributeError,ValueError):return None
def descriptor_type(text,i=0,allow_void=False):
 start=i;dims=0
 while i<len(text) and text[i]=='[':dims+=1;i+=1
 if i>=len(text):raise ValueError('Incomplete JVM descriptor')
 c=text[i]
 if c in PRIMITIVES:
  if c=='V' and (dims or not allow_void):raise ValueError('Illegal void JVM descriptor')
  name=PRIMITIVES[c];i+=1
 elif c=='L':
  end=text.find(';',i)
  if end<0:raise ValueError('Unterminated object JVM descriptor')
  name=text[i+1:end].replace('/','.').replace('$','.');i=end+1
 else:raise ValueError('Unsupported JVM descriptor '+text[start:])
 return name+'[]'*dims,i
def method_descriptor(value):
 p=value.find('(')
 if p<1:raise ValueError('Missing method JVM descriptor')
 name=value[:p];args=[];i=p+1
 while i<len(value) and value[i]!=')':
  t,i=descriptor_type(value,i);args.append(t)
 if i>=len(value):raise ValueError('Unclosed method JVM descriptor')
 result,i=descriptor_type(value,i+1,True)
 if i!=len(value):raise ValueError('Trailing method JVM descriptor')
 return name,args,result
def parse(data,target='37.2'):
 root=ET.fromstring(data);records=[];excluded=[];issues=[];limit=version(target)
 if limit is None:raise ValueError('Numeric platform API major.minor required')
 if root.tag!='api' or root.get('version')!='4':raise ValueError('Unsupported SDK API index format')
 positions=[];locator=xml.parsers.expat.ParserCreate()
 def locate(tag,attrs):
  if tag in ('class','method','field'):positions.append((tag,locator.CurrentLineNumber))
 locator.StartElementHandler=locate;locator.Parse(data,True)
 nodes=[n for n in root.iter() if n.tag in ('class','method','field')]
 assert [n.tag for n in nodes]==[tag for tag,line in positions]
 lines={id(n):line for n,(tag,line) in zip(nodes,positions)}
 def active(node,inherited):
  since=node.get('since',inherited.get('since','1'));removed=node.get('removed',inherited.get('removed'));v=version(since);rv=version(removed) if removed else None
  attrs=dict(inherited);attrs.update(node.attrib)
  known=v is not None and (not removed or rv is not None)
  return known and v<=limit and (rv is None or rv>limit),attrs,known
 for ordinal,c in enumerate(root.findall('class'),1):
  owner_binary=c.get('name','');owner=owner_binary.replace('/','.').replace('$','.');package=owner_binary.rsplit('/',1)[0].replace('/','.') if '/' in owner_binary else ''
  current,life,known=active(c,{})
  for child_index,node in enumerate([c]+[x for x in c if x.tag in ('method','field')]):
   enabled,lifecycle,understood=(current,life,known) if node is c else active(node,life)
   if not current or not enabled:
    excluded.append(dict(owner=owner_binary,tag=node.tag,attributes=node.attrib,reason='not applicable at target platform API' if understood else 'unknown API lifecycle'));continue
   raw=ET.Element(node.tag,node.attrib);raw=ET.tostring(raw,encoding='unicode')
   name=owner.split('.')[-1] if node is c else node.get('name','');kind='unsupported' if node is c else 'field';parameters=[];ret=None
   extra=dict(jvm_owner=owner_binary,api_index_attributes=node.attrib,api_lifecycle=lifecycle,public_sdk_membership='declared-current-in-official-SDK-API-index',target_platform_api=target,source_kind_semantics='published API lifecycle index; not complete Java signature',index_ordinal=ordinal,member_ordinal=child_index)
   canonical=owner_binary if node is c else owner_binary+'#'+name
   if node is c:extra.update(type_kind_status='unknown',parse_error='API index omits interface/class/annotation distinction',supertypes=[dict(x.attrib,relation=x.tag) for x in node if x.tag in ('extends','implements')])
   elif node.tag=='method':
    try:
     name,args,ret=method_descriptor(name);kind='constructor' if name=='<init>' else 'method';parameters=[dict(type=t,name=None,direction=None,annotations=[]) for t in args]
    except ValueError as e:kind='unsupported';extra['parse_error']=str(e);issues.append(dict(owner=owner_binary,name=name,error=str(e)))
   else:extra['field_type_status']='unknown'
   records.append(dict(kind=kind,canonical=canonical,owner=owner,package=package,name=name,return_type=ret,parameters=parameters,annotations=[],all_annotations=[],raw=raw,line=lines[id(node)],extras=extra))
 return dict(records=records,excluded_history_or_future=len(excluded),excluded=excluded,issues=issues,counts_by_kind=dict(collections.Counter(r['kind'] for r in records)),target_platform_api=target,index_format_version=root.get('version'),line_locator_semantics='physical XML line from Expat parser')

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/sdk_zip_metadata.py ===
"""Read selected ZIP metadata members through bounded exact byte ranges.
Never reads android.jar, native libraries or other SDK platform binary payloads.
The caller supplies a range function that must require HTTP206, exact size and
an unchanged ETag. ZIP CRC verifies each selected metadata member.
"""
import io,zipfile
MAX_READ=8*1024*1024
class RangeFile(io.RawIOBase):
 def __init__(self,size,get):self.size=size;self.get=get;self.position=0
 def seekable(self):return True
 def readable(self):return True
 def tell(self):return self.position
 def seek(self,offset,whence=0):
  self.position=offset if whence==0 else self.position+offset if whence==1 else self.size+offset
  if self.position<0 or self.position>self.size:raise ValueError('ZIP seek out of range')
  return self.position
 def read(self,size=-1):
  size=self.size-self.position if size<0 else min(size,self.size-self.position)
  if size>MAX_READ:raise ValueError('Read exceeds metadata byte-range bound')
  b=self.get(self.position,size) if size else b''
  if len(b)!=size:raise ValueError('Short metadata range')
  self.position+=size;return b
def selected(name):
 leaf=name.split('/')[-1]
 return leaf in ('source.properties','sdk.properties','package.xml','api-versions.xml','api-versions-versioned.xml','annotations.zip','public.xml','attrs_manifest.xml','optional.json') or leaf=='AndroidManifest.xml'
def extract_metadata(size,get):
 files={};entries=[]
 with zipfile.ZipFile(RangeFile(size,get)) as archive:
  for i in archive.infolist():
   if i.is_dir():continue
   entries.append(dict(path=i.filename,size_bytes=i.file_size,compressed_bytes=i.compress_size,crc32=f'{i.CRC:08x}',selected=selected(i.filename)))
   if not selected(i.filename):continue
   if i.file_size>32*1024*1024 or i.compress_size>MAX_READ:raise ValueError('Metadata member exceeds bound: '+i.filename)
   files[i.filename]=archive.read(i.filename)
 return files,entries

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/validate_catalog.py ===
"""Read-only validation of every entry, provenance FK, source reconciliation and AST."""
import pathlib, json, sqlite3, hashlib, collections, sys, datetime
import jsonschema
P = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

def run(output, inventory, report_path):
    out = pathlib.Path(output).resolve()
    inv = pathlib.Path(inventory).resolve()
    sources = json.loads(inv.read_text())['sources']
    db = sqlite3.connect('file:' + str(out / 'catalog.sqlite') + '?mode=ro', uri=True)
    validator = jsonschema.Draft202012Validator(json.loads((P / 'contracts/Entry.schema.json').read_text()))
    ast_validator = jsonschema.Draft202012Validator(json.loads((P / 'contracts/PermissionExpression.schema.json').read_text()))
    for f in (P / 'contracts').glob('*.schema.json'):
        jsonschema.Draft202012Validator.check_schema(json.loads(f.read_text()))
    generation = db.execute('SELECT registry_generation FROM catalog_view WHERE singleton=1').fetchone()[0]
    failures = []
    kinds = collections.Counter()
    ops = collections.Counter()
    variants = 0
    n = 0

    def walk(ast):
        ops[ast['op']] += 1
        for v in ast.values():
            if isinstance(v, dict) and 'op' in v:
                walk(v)
            elif isinstance(v, list):
                for x in v:
                    if isinstance(x, dict) and 'op' in x:
                        walk(x)
    for bid, document, digest, etag in db.execute('SELECT entry_id,document_json,record_sha256,etag FROM effective_entries'):
        doc = json.loads(document)
        errors = list(validator.iter_errors(doc))
        if errors:
            failures.extend((dict(entry_id=bid, path=list(e.path), message=e.message) for e in errors))
        if not hashlib.sha256(doc['canonical_signature'].encode()).hexdigest() == bid:
            raise ValueError("Validation failed: hashlib.sha256(doc['canonical_signature'].encode()).hexdigest() == bid")
        if not hashlib.sha256(document.encode()).hexdigest() == digest:
            raise ValueError('Validation failed: hashlib.sha256(document.encode()).hexdigest() == digest')
        if not etag == '"cat1:' + str(generation) + ':' + digest + ':' + bid + ':0:active"':
            raise ValueError('Validation failed: etag == \'"cat1:\' + str(generation) + \':\' + digest + \':\' + bid + \':0:active"\'')
        kinds[doc['kind']] += 1
        walk(doc['permission'])
        variants += 'permission_variants' in doc['extensions']
        n += 1
        for param in doc['extensions'].get('parameters', []):
            if 'permission' in param:
                ast_validator.validate(param['permission'])
        if n % 20000 == 0:
            print('validated entries', n, flush=True)
    if not n == db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM bindings').fetchone()[0]:
        raise ValueError("Validation failed: n == db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM bindings').fetchone()[0]")
    exports = 0
    for line in (out / 'entries.jsonl').open():
        doc = json.loads(line)
        exports += 1
        row = db.execute('SELECT record_sha256 FROM effective_entries WHERE entry_id=?', (doc['entry_id'],)).fetchone()
        if not (row and hashlib.sha256(line.rstrip('\n').encode()).hexdigest() == row[0]):
            raise ValueError("Validation failed: row and hashlib.sha256(line.rstrip('\\n').encode()).hexdigest() == row[0]")
    if not exports == n:
        raise ValueError('Validation failed: exports == n')
    downloaded = [s for s in sources if s['status'] == 'downloaded']
    if not len(downloaded) == db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM sources').fetchone()[0]:
        raise ValueError("Validation failed: len(downloaded) == db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM sources').fetchone()[0]")
    for s in downloaded:
        if not hashlib.sha256((inv.parent / s['local_path']).read_bytes()).hexdigest() == s['sha256']:
            raise ValueError("Validation failed: hashlib.sha256((inv.parent / s['local_path']).read_bytes()).hexdigest() == s['sha256']")
    reports = [json.loads(r[0]) for r in db.execute('SELECT document_json FROM parse_reports')]
    for r in reports:
        if 'expected_declarations' in r:
            if not r['expected_declarations'] == r['declarations']:
                raise ValueError("Validation failed: r['expected_declarations'] == r['declarations']")
    signature_stats = {}
    for k in ('sdk-signature', 'source-signature'):
        rs = [r for r in reports if r['kind'] == k]
        signature_stats[k] = dict(files=len(rs), declarations=sum((r['declarations'] for r in rs)), unsupported=sum((r['unsupported'] for r in rs)))
    integrity = db.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]
    fk = db.execute('PRAGMA foreign_key_check').fetchall()
    if not (integrity == 'ok' and (not fk)):
        raise ValueError("Validation failed: integrity == 'ok' and (not fk)")
    duplicates = db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM (SELECT binding_id,source_id,line,count(*) n FROM occurrences GROUP BY binding_id,source_id,line HAVING n>1)').fetchone()[0]
    if not duplicates == 0:
        raise ValueError('Validation failed: duplicates == 0')
    unmatched = db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM annotation_evidence WHERE binding_id IS NULL').fetchone()[0]
    scoped_annotation_ops = collections.Counter()
    for document, scope in db.execute('SELECT document_json,scope FROM annotation_evidence'):
        a = json.loads(document)['annotation']
        if 'permission' in a:
            ast_validator.validate(a['permission'])
            scoped_annotation_ops[scope + ':' + a['permission']['op']] += 1
    known_defs = {x[0] for x in db.execute('SELECT DISTINCT name FROM permission_definitions')}
    known_symbols = {x[0] for x in db.execute('SELECT DISTINCT value FROM permission_symbols')}
    leaves = collections.Counter()
    for row in db.execute('SELECT expression_json FROM permission_relations'):

        def refs(a):
            if a['op'] == 'permission':
                leaves[a['name']] += 1
            for v in a.values():
                if isinstance(v, dict) and 'op' in v:
                    refs(v)
                elif isinstance(v, list):
                    for x in v:
                        if isinstance(x, dict) and 'op' in x:
                            refs(x)
        refs(json.loads(row[0]))
    result = dict(validated_at=datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(), schema_version='1.2.0', entries_validated=n, json_schema_failures=failures, integrity_check=integrity, foreign_key_check=fk, duplicate_occurrences=duplicates, counts={t: db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM ' + t).fetchone()[0] for t in ('sources', 'bindings', 'occurrences', 'binding_declarations', 'permission_definitions', 'permission_symbols', 'permission_relations', 'aidl_schemas', 'annotation_evidence', 'issues')}, entry_kinds=dict(kinds), effective_permission_nodes=dict(ops), scoped_annotation_permission_nodes=dict(scoped_annotation_ops), source_dependent_permission_entries=variants, signature_reconciliation=signature_stats, external_annotations_unmatched=unmatched, permission_reference_names=dict(total=len(leaves), with_definition=len(set(leaves) & known_defs), constant_only=len((set(leaves) & known_symbols) - known_defs), without_definition_or_constant=len(set(leaves) - known_defs - known_symbols)), undefined_permission_names=sorted(set(leaves) - known_defs - known_symbols), complete=False)
    db.close()
    report_path=pathlib.Path(report_path).resolve()
    if report_path==out or out in report_path.parents:raise ValueError('Validation report must be outside immutable release directory')
    with report_path.open('x') as report:report.write(json.dumps(result,indent=2))
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2), flush=True)
    if failures:
        raise ValueError('JSON Schema validation failed')
    return result
def run_database(database,report_path):
    """Validate a populated v3 database read-only, without exports or acquisition.

    Structural validity is separate from semantic rebuild/source coverage. Legacy
    declaration variants and applicability facts are reported rather than invented.
    """
    path=pathlib.Path(database).resolve();report_path=pathlib.Path(report_path).resolve()
    if path==report_path:raise ValueError('Report cannot replace database')
    db=sqlite3.connect(path.as_uri()+'?mode=ro',uri=True)
    try:
        if db.execute('PRAGMA user_version').fetchone()[0]!=3:raise ValueError('Expected storage v3; migrate legacy storage to a new copy first')
        validator=jsonschema.Draft202012Validator(json.loads((P/'contracts/Entry.schema.json').read_text()))
        ast_validator=jsonschema.Draft202012Validator(json.loads((P/'contracts/PermissionExpression.schema.json').read_text()))
        generation=db.execute('SELECT registry_generation FROM catalog_view').fetchone()[0]
        failures=[];active_entries=0;has_variants={}
        for bid,document,digest,etag in db.execute('SELECT entry_id,document_json,record_sha256,etag FROM effective_entries'):
            doc=json.loads(document);failures.extend(dict(entry_id=bid,path=list(e.path),message=e.message) for e in validator.iter_errors(doc))
            if hashlib.sha256(doc['canonical_signature'].encode()).hexdigest()!=bid:raise ValueError('Canonical entry identity mismatch '+bid)
            if hashlib.sha256(document.encode()).hexdigest()!=digest:raise ValueError('Entry document hash mismatch '+bid)
            if etag!='"cat1:'+str(generation)+':'+digest+':'+bid+':0:active"':raise ValueError('Current entry ETag mismatch '+bid)
            for parameter in doc.get('extensions',{}).get('parameters',[]):
                if 'permission' in parameter:ast_validator.validate(parameter['permission'])
            has_variants[bid]=bool(doc.get('extensions',{}).get('declaration_variants'));active_entries+=1
        for (raw,) in db.execute('SELECT expression_json FROM permission_relations'):ast_validator.validate(json.loads(raw))
        omissions=0;samples=[];previous=None;shapes=set()
        def record_omission(bid,shapes):
            nonlocal omissions
            if bid is not None and len(shapes)>1 and bid in has_variants and not has_variants[bid]:
                omissions+=1
                if len(samples)<20:samples.append(bid)
        for bid,raw in db.execute('SELECT binding_id,document_json FROM binding_declarations ORDER BY binding_id'):
            if previous!=bid:record_omission(previous,shapes);shapes=set();previous=bid
            declaration=json.loads(raw);shape={k:declaration.get(k) for k in ('kind','arguments','return_type')};shapes.add(json.dumps(shape,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':')))
        record_omission(previous,shapes)
        integrity=db.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0];fk=db.execute('PRAGMA foreign_key_check').fetchall()
        rebuilt=db.execute("SELECT value FROM catalog_meta WHERE key='rebuilt_with_corrected_extractor'").fetchone()
        rebuilt=rebuilt[0] if rebuilt else 'unknown'
        result=dict(database=str(path),database_open_mode='read-only',validated_at=datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),entries_validated=active_entries,json_schema_failures=failures,integrity_check=integrity,foreign_key_check=fk,registry_generation=generation,rebuilt_with_corrected_extractor=rebuilt,source_applicability_facts_revalidated=False,declaration_variant_omissions=omissions,declaration_variant_omission_sample_ids=samples,complete=False,limitations=['No source acquisition or semantic rebuild performed','Legacy API/channel claims are preserved, not independently re-established'],counts={t:db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM '+t).fetchone()[0] for t in ('sources','bindings','occurrences','binding_declarations','permission_definitions','registry_entries')})
    finally:db.close()
    with report_path.open('x') as report:json.dump(result,report,indent=2)
    if failures or integrity!='ok' or fk:raise ValueError('Database validation failed; see report')
    if rebuilt=='true' and omissions:raise ValueError('Corrected rebuild omits declaration variants')
    return result

if __name__ == '__main__':
    if sys.argv[1]=='--database':run_database(sys.argv[2],sys.argv[3])
    else:run(sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3])

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tests/test_aidl_members.py ===
import pathlib,sys,unittest
P=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];sys.path.insert(0,str(P/'tooling'))
from parsers import aidl
from aidl_members import enrich
class AidlMembers(unittest.TestCase):
 def parse(self,text):return enrich(text,aidl(text))
 def test_parcelable_fields_annotations_and_values(self):
  result=self.parse('package a; parcelable P {\n @nullable String name;\n int[] values={1,2};\n const String S="a;b";\n}')
  self.assertEqual([m['name'] for m in result['members']],['name','values','S']);self.assertTrue(result['members'][0]['annotations']);self.assertEqual(result['members'][1]['return_type'],'int[]');self.assertEqual(result['members'][2]['extras']['constant_raw'],'"a;b"');self.assertTrue(all(m['extras']['wire_tag'] is None for m in result['members']))
 def test_enum_shifts_and_union_declaration_ordinals_not_tags(self):
  result=self.parse('package a; enum E { A=1<<0, B=1<<1, C, }');self.assertEqual([m['name'] for m in result['members']],['A','B','C']);self.assertEqual(result['members'][1]['extras']['constant_raw'],'1<<1')
  union=self.parse('package a; union U { int x; String y; }');self.assertEqual([m['extras']['declared_ordinal'] for m in union['members']],[0,1]);self.assertTrue(all(m['extras']['wire_tag'] is None for m in union['members']))
 def test_external_parcelable_and_unknown_nested_schema_stay_visible(self):
  self.assertEqual(self.parse('package a; parcelable P cpp_header "P.h";')['members'],[])
  result=self.parse('package a; parcelable P { new_syntax; int good; }');self.assertEqual(result['members'][0]['kind'],'unsupported');self.assertEqual(result['members'][1]['name'],'good');self.assertIn('partial',result['parse_status'])
 def test_interface_constant_is_metadata_and_repeated_lines_are_distinct(self):
  result=self.parse('package a; interface I { const int C=3; void x(); }');self.assertEqual(result['members'][0]['kind'],'field');self.assertEqual(result['members'][0]['name'],'C')
  fields=self.parse('package a; parcelable P {\n int x;\n int y;\n}')['members'];self.assertEqual([m['line'] for m in fields],[2,3])
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

import unittest,pathlib,tempfile,json,hashlib,sqlite3,sys,zipfile

import jsonschema

class BuildTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def fixture(self,r):
  values=[('sdk-signature','api.txt','''// Signature format: 6.0
package android {
 public class Manifest.permission {
 field public static final String CAMERA="android.permission.CAMERA";
 }
}
package a {
 public class A {
 ctor public A();
 method @RequiresPermission(android.Manifest.permission.CAMERA) public void go(int);
 method public void go(String);
 }
}'''),('manifest','AndroidManifest.xml','<manifest xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android"><permission android:name="android.permission.CAMERA" android:protectionLevel="dangerous"/></manifest>'),('aidl','IFoo.aidl','package a; @RequiresNoPermission interface IFoo { @EnforcePermission("android.permission.CAMERA") void go(in int n)=4; }')]
  sources=[]
  for kind,path,text in values:
   data=text.encode();(r/path).write_bytes(data);sources.append(dict(kind=kind,path=path,local_path=path,status='downloaded',sha256=hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),git_blob=hashlib.sha1(b'blob '+str(len(data)).encode()+b'\0'+data).hexdigest(),size_bytes=len(data),url='https://android.googlesource.com/mock/'+path,revision='1'*40,retrieved_at='2026-10-26T00:00:00Z',license='fixture',scope='source',repository='fixture',api_level=37,channel='stable'))
  (r/'inventory.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(catalog=dict(registry_version=1,android_version='fixture',api_level=37,coverage='fixture partial'),sources=sources)));return r/'inventory.json'
 def test_import_integrity_schema_overlay_and_immutability(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   r=pathlib.Path(td);inv=self.fixture(r);v=b.build(inv,r/'out');self.assertEqual(v['integrity_check'],'ok');self.assertEqual(v['foreign_key_check'],[]);self.assertEqual(v['counts']['permission_definitions'],1)
   schema=json.loads((P/'contracts/Entry.schema.json').read_text());validator=jsonschema.Draft202012Validator(schema)
   docs=[json.loads(l) for l in (r/'out/entries.jsonl').read_text().splitlines()]
   for doc in docs:validator.validate(doc)
   self.assertTrue(any(d['permission'].get('name')=='android.permission.CAMERA' for d in docs));self.assertEqual(json.loads((r/'out/catalog.json').read_text())['entries'],docs)
   db=sqlite3.connect(r/'out/catalog.sqlite');db.execute('PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON');bid=db.execute('SELECT entry_id FROM effective_entries LIMIT 1').fetchone()[0]
   with self.assertRaises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):db.execute('UPDATE bindings SET name="changed" WHERE binding_id=?',(bid,))
   count=db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM runtime_catalog').fetchone()[0];now='2026-10-26T00:00:00Z'
   db.execute('INSERT INTO overlays VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(bid,bid,db.execute('SELECT signature FROM bindings WHERE binding_id=?',(bid,)).fetchone()[0],1,None,None,'suppressed','{}',now,now))
   db.execute('INSERT INTO entry_history VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)',(bid,1,'suppress','{}','fixture',now))
   self.assertEqual(db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM runtime_catalog').fetchone()[0],count-1)
   db.execute('UPDATE overlays SET state="active",revision=2,document_json=? WHERE entry_id=?',(json.dumps({'fixture':'override'}),bid));self.assertEqual(json.loads(db.execute('SELECT document_json FROM runtime_catalog WHERE entry_id=?',(bid,)).fetchone()[0]),{'fixture':'override'})
   db.execute('INSERT INTO entry_history VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)',(bid,2,'restore',json.dumps({'fixture':'override'}),'fixture',now))
   db.commit()
   with self.assertRaises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):db.execute('INSERT INTO occurrences VALUES (?,?,?)',('missing','missing',1))
   db.close()
   before={p.name:p.read_bytes() for p in (r/'out').iterdir() if p.is_file()}
   validate_catalog.run(r/'out',inv,r/'validation-report.json')
   self.assertEqual(before,{p.name:p.read_bytes() for p in (r/'out').iterdir() if p.is_file()})
 def test_hash_failure_does_not_publish(self):

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tests/test_contract_models.py ===
import hashlib,itertools,json,pathlib,sqlite3,sys,unittest
P=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];sys.path.insert(0,str(P/'tooling'))
from contract_reference import Catalog,Error,identity
from policy_reference import Rule,evaluate,resolve_fixture_route
from dispatch_manifest import generate

class Models(unittest.TestCase):
    def error(self,status,fn):
        with self.assertRaises(Error) as e:fn()
        self.assertEqual(e.exception.status,status)
    def test_cas_tombstones_history_and_cursor(self):
        c=Catalog();k=c.create('java:a.A#x()',{'v':1});old=c.tag(k);page=c.cursor()
        self.error(428,lambda:c.put(k,None,{}));self.error(400,lambda:c.put(k,'*',{}))
        deleted=c.delete(k,old);self.error(412,lambda:c.put(k,old,{}))
        self.assertEqual(c.delete(k,deleted),deleted)
        c.put(k,deleted,{'v':2});self.assertEqual(c.entries[k]['state'],'active')
        self.error(410,lambda:c.continue_page(page));self.assertEqual(len(c.history[k]),3)
    def test_import_both_collision_orders_and_rebase(self):
        sig='java:a.A#x()';key=identity(sig);base={key:(sig,{'p':1})}
        c=Catalog();c.create(sig,{});c.publish_fixture(base)
        self.assertEqual(c.entries[key]['state'],'conflict');self.error(409,lambda:c.delete(key,c.tag(key)))
        self.error(409,lambda:c.put(key,c.tag(key),{}));c.put(key,c.tag(key),{},dict(action='rebase',expected_imported_record_sha256=c.entries[key]['base_hash']))
        d=Catalog();d.publish_fixture(base);self.error(409,lambda:d.create(sig,{}))
        d.delete(key,d.tag(key));d.publish_fixture({key:(sig,{'p':2})});self.assertEqual(d.entries[key]['state'],'conflict')
        d.publish_fixture({});d.put(key,d.tag(key),{},dict(action='retain-local',expected_imported_record_sha256=None));self.assertFalse(d.entries[key]['imported'])
    def test_removed_base_without_overlay_is_absent(self):
        c=Catalog();sig='java:a.A#x()';key=identity(sig);c.publish_fixture({key:(sig,{})});old=c.tag(key)
        c.publish_fixture({});self.assertNotIn(key,c.entries);self.error(404,lambda:c.put(key,old,{}))
    def test_rejected_registry_does_not_mutate_selected_view(self):
        import copy
        c=Catalog();sig='java:a.A#x()';key=identity(sig);c.publish_fixture({key:(sig,{})});before=copy.deepcopy(c.entries);cursor=c.cursor()
        self.error(422,lambda:c.publish_fixture({'wrong-id':(sig,{})}));self.assertEqual(c.entries,before);self.assertEqual(c.cursor(),cursor)
    def test_binding_rules_dominate_all_aliases(self):
        aliases={'generated_x':('binding','s')};schemas={'binding':'s'};wire={('fixture.I',42):('binding','s')}
        requests=[('generated',dict(tool_name='generated_x',target='t',items=2)),('generic',dict(binding_id='binding',schema_hash='s',target='t',items=2)),('raw',dict(descriptor='fixture.I',transaction_code=42,decoded_and_validated=True,target='t',items=2))]
        plans=[resolve_fixture_route(route,request,aliases,schemas,wire) for route,request in requests]
        self.assertEqual(plans[0],plans[1]);self.assertEqual(plans[0],plans[2]);plan=plans[0]
        for effect,entry_effect,authority,emergency in itertools.product(('deny','ask','allow'),('deny','ask','allow'),(False,True),(False,True)):
            rule=Rule(effect,'s',frozenset({'t'}),3);entry=Rule(entry_effect,'tool',frozenset({'t'}),3)
            decisions=[evaluate(p,{'binding':rule},entry,entry_schema='tool',authority=authority,emergency=emergency) for p in plans]
            self.assertEqual(len(set(decisions)),1)
            if effect=='deny' or entry_effect=='deny' or emergency:self.assertEqual(decisions[0],'deny')
        allow=Rule('allow','s',frozenset({'t'}),1)
        self.assertEqual(evaluate(plan,{'binding':allow},allow,entry_schema='s',authority=True),'deny')
        self.assertEqual(evaluate(plan,{},allow,entry_schema='s',authority=True),'deny')
        self.assertEqual(evaluate([('raw:service','s','t',1)],{'binding':allow},allow,entry_schema='s',authority=True),'deny')
        self.assertEqual(evaluate([('binding','s','t',1)],{'binding':allow},allow,entry_schema='stale-tool-schema',authority=True),'deny')
        for raw in (dict(descriptor='fixture.I',transaction_code=42,target='t',items=1),dict(descriptor='fixture.I',transaction_code=99,target='t',items=1)):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):resolve_fixture_route('raw',raw,aliases,schemas,wire)
    def test_generated_alias_identity_and_default_deny(self):
        sig='java:a.A#x()';e=dict(entry_id=identity(sig),canonical_signature=sig)
        r=generate([e])['aliases'][0];self.assertEqual(r['binding_id'],r['generic_binding_id']);self.assertEqual(r['default_grant'],'deny')
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):generate([e,e])
    def test_bridge_ddl_integrity_and_closed_enums(self):
        db=sqlite3.connect(':memory:');db.executescript((P/'bridge-config.sql').read_text())
        db.execute("INSERT INTO clients VALUES('fixture','fixture','active',0,1,0,0,NULL)")
        with self.assertRaises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):db.execute("INSERT INTO credentials VALUES('fixture','missing',zeroblob(32),'fixture','active',0,NULL,NULL,0)")
        with self.assertRaises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):db.execute("INSERT INTO client_logging_config(client_id,mode,paused,logging_revision,retention_seconds,byte_quota,record_byte_cap,redaction_profile_version,updated_at) VALUES('fixture','full-raw',0,1,1,1,1,1,0)")
        self.assertEqual(db.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0],'ok');self.assertEqual(db.execute('PRAGMA foreign_key_check').fetchall(),[])

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

import unittest,pathlib,sys
sys.path.insert(0,str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'tooling'))
import java_context
class JavaContextTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_imports_retained_in_runtime_source_details(self):
  sources=[dict(kind='java-source',path='C.java',metadata_cache=dict(path='cache.gz',sha256='abc',parser_sha256='def'))]
  stats=[dict(cache_path='cache.gz',cache_sha256='abc',parser_sha256='def',package='a',imports={'List':'java.util.List'},wildcard_imports=['other'],parser='tested',parse_errors=0)]
  java_context.attach(sources,stats)
  self.assertEqual(sources[0]['java_file_context']['imports'],{'List':'java.util.List'})
  self.assertEqual(sources[0]['java_file_context']['wildcard_imports'],['other'])
 def test_mismatched_context_rejected(self):
  sources=[dict(kind='java-source',path='C.java',metadata_cache=dict(path='cache.gz',sha256='abc',parser_sha256='def'))]
  with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'context cache mismatch'):java_context.attach(sources,[dict(cache_path='cache.gz',cache_sha256='wrong',parser_sha256='def')])

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tests/test_java_source.py ===
"""SDK source AST acceptance fixtures, written before Java-source parser."""
import unittest,pathlib,sys
sys.path.insert(0,str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'tooling'))
try:import java_source
except ImportError:java_source=None
class JavaSourceTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def setUp(self):self.assertIsNotNone(java_source,'Java source metadata parser not implemented yet')
 def test_nested_overloads_fields_and_annotations(self):
  text='''package a; import java.util.List; import android.annotation.RequiresPermission;
  public class Outer<T> {
   public static final String P="android.permission.CAMERA", Q="android.permission.RECORD_AUDIO";
   public Outer() {}
   @RequiresPermission(value="android.permission.CAMERA",conditional=true)
   public List<String> go(@Deprecated final String x, int... ns) { return null; }
   public void go(int n) {}
   public static class Inner { public int value; }
   public @interface A { String value() default ""; }
  }'''
  result=java_source.parse(text,'Fixture.java');rs=result['records'];self.assertEqual(result['parse_errors'],0)
  self.assertIn('a.Outer.Inner',{r['owner'] for r in rs});self.assertEqual(sum(r['name']=='go' for r in rs),2)
  self.assertEqual({r['name'] for r in rs if r['kind']=='field' and r['owner']=='a.Outer'},{'P','Q'})
  method=next(r for r in rs if r['name']=='go' and len(r['parameters'])==2)
  self.assertEqual(method['parameters'][1]['type'],'int...');self.assertTrue(method['parameters'][0]['annotations'])
  self.assertEqual(method['annotations'][0]['name'],'android.annotation.RequiresPermission');self.assertTrue(any(r['kind']=='annotation' for r in rs))
 def test_method_body_local_types_not_public_surface(self):
  rs=java_source.parse('package a; public class C { public void x(){ class Local { int y; } } }','C.java')['records']
  self.assertFalse(any(r['name']=='Local' for r in rs));self.assertFalse(any(r['name']=='y' for r in rs))
 def test_interface_implicit_public_and_directions_unknown(self):
  rs=java_source.parse('package a; public interface I { String NAME="p"; void f(String p); }','I.java')['records']
  self.assertEqual(next(r for r in rs if r['name']=='f')['extras']['effective_visibility'],'public')
  self.assertIsNone(next(r for r in rs if r['name']=='f')['parameters'][0]['direction'])
 def test_parse_error_retained(self):
  r=java_source.parse('package a; public class X { public void broken( }','X.java');self.assertGreater(r['parse_errors'],0);self.assertTrue(r['issues'])
 def test_enum_and_multiple_array_declarators(self):
  rs=java_source.parse('package a; public enum E { RED,BLUE; public int a[],b; }','E.java')['records']
  self.assertEqual({r['name'] for r in rs if r['extras'].get('enum_constant')},{'RED','BLUE'})
  self.assertEqual(next(r for r in rs if r['kind']=='field' and r['name']=='a')['return_type'],'int[]');self.assertEqual(next(r for r in rs if r['kind']=='field' and r['name']=='b')['return_type'],'int')
 def test_repeated_large_line_numbers(self):
  text='\n'*300+'package a;\npublic class C {\n public int x;\n public void f(int n){}\n}'
  for _ in range(100):
   rows=java_source.parse(text,'Large.java')['records']
   self.assertEqual(next(r for r in rows if r['kind']=='field')['line'],303)
 def test_annotation_like_comment_is_not_metadata(self):
  rows=java_source.parse('package a; public /* @NotAnAnnotation( */ class C { public /* @other( */ int x; }','Comments.java')['records']
  self.assertEqual(next(r for r in rows if r['kind']=='field')['name'],'x')
  self.assertFalse(any(r['annotations'] for r in rows))
 def test_apostrophe_in_annotation_comment(self):
  text='''package a; @LongDef(value={1, // because it'd wrap\n 2}) public @interface Bits {}'''
  rows=java_source.parse(text,'Bits.java')['records']
  annotation=next(r for r in rows if r['kind']=='annotation')
  self.assertEqual(len(annotation['annotations']),1)
  self.assertIn('1,',annotation['annotations'][0]['arguments'])
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tests/test_jvm_source_link.py ===
import unittest,pathlib,sys
sys.path.insert(0,str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'tooling'))
import jvm_source_link as link
class JvmLinkTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_exact_owner_primitives_and_known_reference_type(self):
  owners={'a.C':'a/C','java.lang.String':'java/lang/String'}
  r=dict(kind='method',owner='a.C',name='go',return_type='void',parameters=[dict(type='java.lang.String[]'),dict(type='int')],extras=dict(owner_binary_name='a/C'))
  self.assertEqual(link.key(r,owners),'a/C#go([Ljava/lang/String;I)V')
 def test_unresolved_type_variable_never_guessed(self):
  r=dict(kind='method',owner='a.C',name='go',return_type='T',parameters=[],extras=dict(owner_binary_name='a/C'))
  self.assertIsNone(link.key(r,{'a.C':'a/C'}))
 def test_unqualified_java_lang_name_might_be_shadowed(self):
  self.assertIsNone(link.encode('String','a.C',{'java.lang.String':'java/lang/String'}))
 def test_untyped_index_field_cannot_prove_typed_jvm_identity(self):
  r=dict(kind='field',owner='a.C.Inner',name='X',extras=dict(owner_binary_name='a/C$Inner'))
  self.assertIsNone(link.key(r,{'a.C.Inner':'a/C$Inner'}))
