=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tests/test_parsers.py ===
import unittest,pathlib,sys,json
sys.path.insert(0,str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'tooling'))
import parsers as p
class ParserTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_overloads_and_annotations(self):
  text='''package a {
 public @interface Ann {
 method public abstract String value();
 }
 public class Outer.Inner<T> {
 ctor public Outer.Inner();
 method @RequiresPermission("p") public <T extends java.lang.Object> java.util.List<T> go(@Nullable java.util.Map<String,java.util.List<T>>, int);
 method public void go(int);
 field public static final String S = "hello // no comment";
 }
 }'''
  rs=list(p.signature(text));self.assertFalse(any(r['kind']=='unsupported' for r in rs));self.assertEqual(rs[1]['kind'],'annotation');ms=[x for x in rs if x['name']=='go'];self.assertNotEqual(ms[0]['canonical'],ms[1]['canonical']);self.assertEqual(ms[0]['parameters'][0]['type'],'java.util.Map<String,java.util.List<T>>');self.assertEqual(ms[0]['return_type'],'java.util.List<T>');self.assertEqual(p.permission(ms[0]['annotations'])['name'],'p')
 def test_permission_modes(self):
  for text,op in [('@RequiresPermission(value="x")','permission'),('@RequiresPermission(allOf={"a","b"})','all'),('@RequiresPermission(anyOf={"a","b"},conditional=true)','conditional'),('@RequiresPermission.Read(@RequiresPermission("x"))','access'),('@PermissionManuallyEnforced','manual'),('@RequiresNoPermission','explicit-none'),('','unknown')]:self.assertEqual(p.permission(p.annotations(text)[1])['op'],op)
  self.assertEqual(p.permission(p.annotations('@RequiresPermission(android.Manifest.permission.X)')[1],{'android.Manifest.permission.X':'android.permission.X'})['name'],'android.permission.X')
  self.assertEqual(p.permission(p.annotations('@EnforcePermission("X")')[1],aidl=True)['op'],'unknown')
  self.assertEqual(p.permission(p.annotations('@RequiresPermission("carrier privileges")')[1])['op'],'unknown')
  self.assertEqual(p.permission(p.annotations('@RequiresPermission.Read')[1])['op'],'access')
 def test_parameter_permission_not_callable(self):
  r=p.member('method public void x(@RequiresPermission("p") String);','a.A','a');self.assertEqual(p.permission(r['annotations'])['op'],'unknown');self.assertTrue(r['parameters'][0]['annotations'])
 def test_aidl(self):
  text='''package a; import b.B;
 @EnforcePermission("android.permission.X") oneway interface IFoo {
 @RequiresNoPermission void ping(in B b, out int[] value) = 7;
 @PermissionManuallyEnforced oneway void send(in String s);
 const int C=3;
 }'''
  d=p.aidl(text);self.assertEqual(d['descriptor'],'a.IFoo');self.assertTrue(d['oneway']);self.assertEqual(len(d['members']),3);self.assertEqual(d['members'][0]['extras']['declared_method_id'],7);self.assertIsNone(d['members'][0]['extras']['wire_transaction_code']);self.assertEqual(d['members'][0]['parameters'][1]['direction'],'out');self.assertEqual(d['members'][2]['kind'],'unsupported');self.assertEqual(p.permission(d['annotations'],aidl=True)['name'],'android.permission.X')
 def test_source_unknown_retained(self):
  r=list(p.signature('package a {\n strange new declaration;\n}'));self.assertEqual(r[1]['kind'],'unsupported');self.assertEqual(r[1]['raw'],'strange new declaration;')
 def test_owner_reset_after_type(self):
  rs=list(p.signature('package a {\n public class A {\n method public void x();\n }\n method public void orphan();\n}'))
  self.assertEqual(rs[-1]['kind'],'unsupported');self.assertIn('without owner',rs[-1]['extras']['parse_error'])
 def test_comment_quoted(self):self.assertIn('"//x"',p.without_comments('const String X="//x"; // hi'))
 def test_aidl_structured(self):
  d=p.aidl('package a; @JavaDerive(toString=true) parcelable P { int n; }');self.assertEqual(d['descriptor'],'a.P');self.assertIn('retained',d['parse_status'])
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tests/test_remediation.py ===
"""Regression evidence for recovered Claude findings; offline fixtures only."""
import json, pathlib, sqlite3, sys, tempfile, unittest
P=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0,str(P/'tooling'))
import parsers
import jsonschema

def database():
    db=sqlite3.connect(':memory:')
    for p in sorted((P/'migrations').glob('*.sql')): db.executescript(p.read_text())
    return db

class ContractRegressions(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_all_natural_primary_keys_are_not_null(self):
        db=database();self.addCleanup(db.close)
        violations=[]
        for (table,) in db.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'"):
            for _,name,typ,nn,_,pk in db.execute('PRAGMA table_info('+table+')'):
                if pk and typ!='INTEGER' and not nn: violations.append(table+'.'+name)
        self.assertEqual(violations,[])

    def test_registry_state_round_trip(self):
        db=database();self.addCleanup(db.close)
        states=['staged','validated','rejected','published','retired']
        for n,state in enumerate(states):
            db.execute('INSERT INTO registries(registry_id,registry_version,schema_version,android_version,api_level,source_set_hash,created_at,state,validation_json) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(str(n),n+1,'1.2.0','fixture',37,'x','now',state,'{}'))
            schema=json.loads((P/'contracts/Entry.schema.json').read_text())
            jsonschema.Draft202012Validator({'$ref':'#/$defs/Registry','$defs':schema['$defs']}).validate(dict(registry_id=str(n),registry_version=n+1,schema_version='1.2.0',android_version='fixture',api_level=37,source_set_hash='0'*64,state=state,validation={}))
        self.assertEqual([x[0] for x in db.execute('SELECT state FROM registries ORDER BY registry_version')],states)

    def test_operation_specific_preconditions(self):
        api=json.loads((P/'contracts/openapi.json').read_text())
        search=api['paths']['/v1/catalog/entries']['get']
        self.assertNotIn('412',search['responses']);self.assertNotIn('428',search['responses'])
        entry=api['paths']['/v1/catalog/entries/{entry_id}']
        for verb in ('put','delete'):
            header=next(p for p in entry[verb]['parameters'] if p['name']=='If-Match')
            self.assertFalse(header['required']);self.assertIn('428',header['description']);self.assertIn('428',entry[verb]['responses'])
        self.assertIn('410',api['paths']['/v1/catalog/entries/{entry_id}/history']['get']['responses'])

    def test_registry_switch_selects_immutable_entry_variant(self):
        db=database();self.addCleanup(db.close)
        db.execute("INSERT INTO bindings(binding_id,signature,package,kind,name,raw_signature) VALUES('b','java:a.A','a','class','A','fixture')")
        for n in (1,2):
            db.execute('INSERT INTO registries VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(str(n),n+1,'1.2.0','fixture',37,'hash','now','published' if n==1 else 'validated','{}'))
            db.execute('INSERT INTO registry_entries VALUES(?,?,?,?)',(str(n),'b',json.dumps({'version':n}),str(n)*64))
        db.execute("INSERT INTO active_registry VALUES(1,'1')")
        first=db.execute('SELECT document_json,etag FROM effective_entries').fetchone()
        db.execute("UPDATE registries SET state='retired' WHERE registry_id='1'")
        db.execute("UPDATE registries SET state='published' WHERE registry_id='2'")
        db.execute("UPDATE active_registry SET registry_id='2' WHERE singleton=1")
        db.commit()
        second=db.execute('SELECT document_json,etag FROM effective_entries').fetchone()
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(first[0])['version'],1);self.assertEqual(json.loads(second[0])['version'],2);self.assertNotEqual(first[1],second[1])
        with self.assertRaises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):db.execute("UPDATE registry_entries SET document_json='{}'")

    def test_conflict_resolution_extension_schema(self):
        schema=json.loads((P/'contracts/Mutation.schema.json').read_text());validator=jsonschema.Draft202012Validator(schema)
        doc=dict(schema_version='1.0.0',canonical_signature='java:a.A',kind='class',name='A',permission=dict(op='unknown',reason='fixture'),codec_status='unknown',unresolved=[],required_semantics=[],reason='fixture')
        for resolution in (dict(action='rebase',expected_imported_record_sha256='0'*64),dict(action='retain-local',expected_imported_record_sha256=None)):
            validator.validate(dict(doc,**{'x-resolution':resolution}))
        with self.assertRaises(jsonschema.ValidationError):validator.validate(dict(doc,**{'x-resolution':dict(action='rebase',expected_imported_record_sha256=None)}))

    def test_permission_root_vocabulary(self):
        db=database();self.addCleanup(db.close)
        ddl=db.execute("SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE name='permission_relations'").fetchone()[0]
        self.assertNotIn("'value'",ddl)
        self.assertIn("'access'",ddl)
        self.assertIn("'permission'",ddl)

    def test_offline_copy_migration(self):
        from migrate_v3 import migrate
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
            r=pathlib.Path(td);old=sqlite3.connect(r/'old.sqlite')
            for name in ('001-initial.sql','002-evidence-and-publication.sql'):
                old.executescript((P.parent/'evidence'/('original-'+name)).read_text())
            old.execute("INSERT INTO registries VALUES('fixture',1,'1.1.0','fixture',37,'hash','now','published','{}')");old.execute('ANALYZE');old.commit();old.close()
            before=(r/'old.sqlite').read_bytes();migrate(r/'old.sqlite',r/'new.sqlite')
            self.assertEqual(before,(r/'old.sqlite').read_bytes())
            new=sqlite3.connect(r/'new.sqlite');self.addCleanup(new.close);self.assertEqual(new.execute('SELECT state FROM registries').fetchone()[0],'published')
            self.assertEqual(new.execute('PRAGMA user_version').fetchone()[0],3)

class ParserRegressions(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_symbolic_read_and_header_do_not_leak_owner(self):
        rs=list(parsers.signature('package a {\n public @interface Ann {\n method @RequiresPermission.Read(@RequiresPermission(android.Manifest.permission.CAMERA)) public String x();\n }\n method public void orphan();\n}'))
        self.assertEqual(rs[1]['kind'],'annotation')
        ast=parsers.permission(rs[2]['annotations'],{'android.Manifest.permission.CAMERA':'android.permission.CAMERA'})
        self.assertEqual(ast['requirement']['name'],'android.permission.CAMERA')
        self.assertEqual(rs[-1]['kind'],'unsupported')

    def test_repeated_aidl_statement_locators_are_distinct(self):
        d=parsers.aidl('package a; interface I {\n void x();\n void x();\n}')
        self.assertNotEqual(d['members'][0]['line'],d['members'][1]['line'])

if __name__=='__main__': unittest.main()

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tests/test_restore_snapshot.py ===
import unittest,pathlib,sys,tempfile,hashlib,json,zipfile
from unittest.mock import patch
sys.path.insert(0,str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'tooling'))
import restore_snapshot as restore
class RestoreTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_source_zip_member_verified_without_network_or_install(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   root=pathlib.Path(td);archive=root/'sources.zip'
   with zipfile.ZipFile(archive,'w') as z:z.writestr('src/a/C.java','package a; class C{}')
   data=archive.read_bytes();s=dict(transport=dict(archive_local_path='sources.zip',archive_sha256=hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),archive_sha1=hashlib.sha1(data).hexdigest(),archive_member='src/a/C.java'))
   with patch.object(restore,'get',side_effect=AssertionError('Network forbidden in fixture')):
    self.assertEqual(restore.restore_member(s,root),b'package a; class C{}')
   archive.write_bytes(b'tampered')
   with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'Existing source archive hash mismatch'):restore.restore_member(s,root)
 def test_missing_source_default_is_dry_run(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   root=pathlib.Path(td);inv=root/'inventory.json';inv.write_text(json.dumps(dict(sources=[dict(status='downloaded',git_blob='1'*40,local_path='snapshots/missing')])) )
   with patch.object(restore,'get',side_effect=AssertionError('No network during dry run')):restore.main(inv)
   self.assertFalse((root/'snapshots/missing').exists())

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tests/test_round2.py ===
import pathlib,sys,json,sqlite3,tempfile,unittest,hashlib,zipfile,copy,subprocess,xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
P=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];sys.path.insert(0,str(P/'tooling'))
import build_catalog as b,parsers,dispatch_manifest,policy_reference,foundation_extract
import test_build

class Round2(unittest.TestCase):
 def fixture(self,r):return test_build.BuildTests().fixture(r)
 def test_source_metadata_and_generation_round_trip(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   r=pathlib.Path(td);inv=self.fixture(r);data=json.loads(inv.read_text());data['catalog']=dict(registry_version=7,android_version='fixture-release',api_level=37,coverage='partial-fixture')
   for n,s in enumerate(data['sources']):s.update(api_level=35+n,channel='preview' if n==0 else 'stable')
   inv.write_text(json.dumps(data));b.build(inv,r/'one');data['catalog']['registry_version']=8;inv.write_text(json.dumps(data));b.build(inv,r/'two')
   first=sqlite3.connect(r/'one/catalog.sqlite');second=sqlite3.connect(r/'two/catalog.sqlite');self.addCleanup(first.close);self.addCleanup(second.close)
   self.assertEqual(first.execute("SELECT api_level,channel FROM sources WHERE path='api.txt'").fetchone(),(35,'preview'))
   self.assertEqual(first.execute('SELECT registry_version FROM registries').fetchone()[0],7)
   self.assertNotEqual(first.execute('SELECT etag FROM effective_entries LIMIT 1').fetchone()[0],second.execute('SELECT etag FROM effective_entries LIMIT 1').fetchone()[0])
 def test_missing_inventory_metadata_fails_closed(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   r=pathlib.Path(td);inv=self.fixture(r);data=json.loads(inv.read_text());data.pop('catalog',None);inv.write_text(json.dumps(data))
   with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'catalog metadata'):b.build(inv,r/'out')

 def test_corrected_source_fact_gets_new_occurrence_not_new_binding(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   r=pathlib.Path(td);inv=self.fixture(r);data=json.loads(inv.read_text());b.build(inv,r/'one')
   data['catalog']['registry_version']=2;data['sources'][0]['api_level']=35;inv.write_text(json.dumps(data));b.build(inv,r/'two')
   first=sqlite3.connect(r/'one/catalog.sqlite');second=sqlite3.connect(r/'two/catalog.sqlite');self.addCleanup(first.close);self.addCleanup(second.close)
   self.assertNotEqual(first.execute("SELECT source_id FROM sources WHERE path='api.txt'").fetchone(),second.execute("SELECT source_id FROM sources WHERE path='api.txt'").fetchone())
   self.assertEqual(first.execute("SELECT binding_id FROM bindings WHERE signature='java:a.A#go(int)'").fetchone(),second.execute("SELECT binding_id FROM bindings WHERE signature='java:a.A#go(int)'").fetchone())

 def test_package_and_type_have_distinct_identity_namespaces(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   r=pathlib.Path(td);inv=self.fixture(r);data=json.loads(inv.read_text());s=data['sources'][0];raw=((r/'api.txt').read_text()+'\npackage a {\n public class B {\n method public void x();\n }\n}\npackage a.B {\n public class C {\n method public void y();\n }\n}\n').encode();(r/'api.txt').write_bytes(raw);s.update(sha256=hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),git_blob=hashlib.sha1(b'blob '+str(len(raw)).encode()+b'\0'+raw).hexdigest(),size_bytes=len(raw));inv.write_text(json.dumps(data));b.build(inv,r/'out')
   db=sqlite3.connect(r/'out/catalog.sqlite');self.addCleanup(db.close)
   type_id=db.execute("SELECT binding_id FROM bindings WHERE signature='java:a.B' AND kind='class'").fetchone()[0]
   package_id=db.execute("SELECT binding_id FROM bindings WHERE signature='java:package:a.B' AND kind='package'").fetchone()[0]
   self.assertNotEqual(type_id,package_id)
 def test_failed_header_does_not_pop_package(self):
  rs=list(parsers.signature('package a {\n public record Bad {\n }\n public class Good {\n method public void x();\n }\n}'))
  self.assertTrue(any(r['canonical']=='a.Good#x()' and r['kind']=='method' for r in rs))
 def test_legacy_extractor_has_no_executable_run(self):self.assertFalse(hasattr(foundation_extract,'run'))
 def test_dispatch_schema_is_independent_of_provenance(self):
  sig='java:a.A#x()';doc=dict(entry_id=hashlib.sha256(sig.encode()).hexdigest(),canonical_signature=sig,arguments=[],codec_status='unknown')
  before=dispatch_manifest.generate([doc])['aliases'][0];after=dispatch_manifest.generate([dict(doc,provenance=[dict(source_id='new')])])['aliases'][0]
  self.assertEqual(before['schema_hash'],after['schema_hash']);self.assertNotEqual(before['document_hash'],after['document_hash'])
 def test_bridge_config_matches_named_design_records(self):
  db=sqlite3.connect(':memory:');self.addCleanup(db.close);db.executescript((P/'bridge-config.sql').read_text())
  tables={x[0] for x in db.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
  self.assertTrue({'resource_grants','bridge_config','client_logging_config','client_call_logs','logging_counters','target_app_profiles','client_raw_grants'}<=tables)
  columns={x[1] for x in db.execute('PRAGMA table_info(client_call_logs)')};self.assertIn('payload_ciphertext',columns);self.assertNotIn('projection_json',columns)
  cols={x[1] for x in db.execute('PRAGMA table_info(executions)')};self.assertTrue({'connection_profile_id','target_id','backend_kind','dispatch_time'}<=cols)
  expected={
   'resource_grants':'handle_id client_id exact_uri_ciphertext key_alias modes enabled revision created_at',
   'bridge_config':'singleton_id row_version desired_state start_on_boot last_stop_reason fgs_type transport_profile loopback_address port_preference server_identity_key_alias retention_days audit_size_cap',
   'client_logging_config':'client_id mode paused logging_revision retention_seconds byte_quota record_byte_cap diagnostic_expires_at diagnostic_boot_id redaction_profile_version updated_at',
   'client_call_logs':'log_id client_id call_id tool_id backend_kind started_at finished_at decision_code outcome_code policy_revision logging_revision expires_at size_bytes payload_ciphertext key_alias',
   'logging_counters':'client_id optional_dropped_count quota_dropped_count last_drop_at',
   'connection_profiles':'profile_id backend_kind display_name enabled row_version device_serial_or_binding android_user_id endpoint_metadata_json peer_fingerprint verification_strength secret_ciphertext wrapping_key_alias paired_at revoked_at',
   'target_app_profiles':'target_id connection_profile_id package_name signing_identity identity_verification_method runtime_protocol runtime_endpoint server_identity enabled row_version'}
  for table,names in expected.items():self.assertTrue(set(names.split())<={r[1] for r in db.execute('PRAGMA table_info('+table+')')},table)
  indexes=[r[0] for r in db.execute("SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='index' AND sql IS NOT NULL")]
  self.assertTrue(any('WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL' in sql and 'UNIQUE' in sql for sql in indexes))
  db.executescript((P/'schema.sql').read_text());self.assertEqual(db.execute('PRAGMA foreign_key_check').fetchall(),[])
 def test_scoped_idempotency_storage(self):

  for principal in ('alice','bob'):db.execute('INSERT INTO idempotency_records VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(principal,'create','same-key','a'*64,201,'{}','resource'))
  self.assertEqual(db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM idempotency_records').fetchone()[0],2)
 def test_external_annotations_scope_mapping_and_parse_error(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   r=pathlib.Path(td);inv=self.fixture(r);data=json.loads(inv.read_text());path=r/'annotations.zip'
   root=ET.Element('root')
   for target in ('a.A void go(int)','a.A void go(int) 0','a.Missing void x()'):
    item=ET.SubElement(root,'item',name=target);ann=ET.SubElement(item,'annotation',name='android.annotation.RequiresPermission');ET.SubElement(ann,'val',name='value',val='"android.permission.CAMERA"')
   with zipfile.ZipFile(path,'w') as z:z.writestr('valid.xml',ET.tostring(root));z.writestr('malformed.xml','<bad')
   raw=path.read_bytes();s=dict(data['sources'][0]);s.update(kind='sdk-annotations',path=path.name,local_path=path.name,url='https://android.googlesource.com/mock/'+path.name,sha256=hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),git_blob=hashlib.sha1(b'blob '+str(len(raw)).encode()+b'\0'+raw).hexdigest(),size_bytes=len(raw));data['sources'].append(s);inv.write_text(json.dumps(data));b.build(inv,r/'out')
   db=sqlite3.connect(r/'out/catalog.sqlite');self.addCleanup(db.close)
   self.assertEqual(db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM annotation_evidence').fetchone()[0],3)
   self.assertEqual(db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM annotation_evidence WHERE binding_id IS NULL').fetchone()[0],1)
   self.assertEqual(db.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM permission_relations WHERE annotation='external'").fetchone()[0],1)
   self.assertEqual(db.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM issues WHERE category='annotation-xml'").fetchone()[0],1)

 def test_positive_raw_grant_never_overrides_known_binding(self):
  rule=policy_reference.Rule('ask','s',frozenset({'t'}),2);entry=policy_reference.Rule('allow','tool',frozenset({'t'}),2)
  grant=policy_reference.RawGrant('c','fixture.I',1,99,1,'h',rule,frozenset({'h1'}),frozenset({0}));req=dict(connection_id='c',descriptor='fixture.I',transaction_code=50,contract_hash='h',flags=1,target='t',items=1,handle='h1',display_id=0)
  args=dict(entry_schema='tool',authority=True,wire_mappings={},schemas={},binding_rules={})
  self.assertEqual(policy_reference.evaluate_raw_fixture(req,{('c','fixture.I'):grant},entry,**args),'ask')
  allow=policy_reference.RawGrant('c','fixture.I',1,99,1,'h',policy_reference.Rule('allow','s',frozenset({'t'}),2),frozenset({'h1'}),frozenset({0}))
  self.assertEqual(policy_reference.evaluate_raw_fixture(req,{('c','fixture.I'):allow},entry,**args),'admit-candidate')
  self.assertEqual(policy_reference.evaluate_raw_fixture(dict(req,flags=2),{('c','fixture.I'):allow},entry,**args),'deny')
  self.assertEqual(policy_reference.evaluate_raw_fixture(dict(req,handle='foreign'),{('c','fixture.I'):allow},entry,**args),'deny')
  self.assertEqual(policy_reference.evaluate_raw_fixture(dict(req,display_id=1),{('c','fixture.I'):allow},entry,**args),'deny')
  args.update(wire_mappings={('fixture.I',50):('known','s')},schemas={'known':'s'})
  self.assertEqual(policy_reference.evaluate_raw_fixture(dict(req,decoded_and_validated=True),{('c','fixture.I'):allow},entry,**args),'deny')

 def test_overlay_revision_identity_and_registry_state_constraints(self):
  db=sqlite3.connect(':memory:');self.addCleanup(db.close);db.executescript((P/'schema.sql').read_text())
  db.execute("INSERT INTO bindings(binding_id,signature,package,kind,name,raw_signature) VALUES('b','java:a.A','a','class','A','fixture')")
  with self.assertRaises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):db.execute("INSERT INTO overlays VALUES('wrong','b','java:a.A',1,NULL,NULL,'active','{}','now','now')")
  with self.assertRaises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):db.execute("INSERT INTO overlays VALUES('b','b','wrong',1,NULL,NULL,'active','{}','now','now')")
  db.execute("INSERT INTO overlays VALUES('b','b','java:a.A',1,NULL,NULL,'active','{}','now','now')")
  with self.assertRaises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):db.execute("UPDATE overlays SET document_json='{\"changed\":true}'")
  db.execute("INSERT INTO registries VALUES('r',1,'1.2.0','fixture',37,'h','now','retired','{}')")
  with self.assertRaises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):db.execute("UPDATE registries SET state='published'")

 def test_minor_schema_and_declaration_variants(self):
  import jsonschema
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tests/test_sdk_api_index.py ===
import unittest,pathlib,sys
sys.path.insert(0,str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'tooling'))
import sdk_api_index as api
class ApiIndexTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_minor_lifecycle_and_removed_members(self):
  xml=b'''<api version="4"><class name="a/Outer$Inner" since="37.1"><method name="&lt;init>()V"/><method name="f([Ljava/lang/String;I)J" since="37.2"/><field name="OLD" removed="37.2"/><field name="FUTURE" since="37.3"/></class></api>'''
  result=api.parse(xml,'37.2');rs=result['records']
  self.assertEqual(len(rs),3);self.assertEqual(result['excluded_history_or_future'],2)
  method=next(r for r in rs if r['name']=='f');self.assertEqual(method['return_type'],'long')
  self.assertEqual([p['type'] for p in method['parameters']],['java.lang.String[]','int'])
  self.assertEqual(method['owner'],'a.Outer.Inner');self.assertEqual(method['extras']['jvm_owner'],'a/Outer$Inner')
 def test_field_type_and_type_kind_are_not_invented(self):
  rows=api.parse(b'<api version="4"><class name="android/Manifest$permission"><field name="CAMERA"/></class></api>','37.2')['records']
  self.assertEqual(rows[0]['kind'],'unsupported');self.assertIsNone(rows[1]['return_type'])
  self.assertEqual(rows[1]['extras']['field_type_status'],'unknown')
 def test_invalid_method_descriptor_retained_unknown(self):
  rows=api.parse(b'<api version="4"><class name="a/C"><method name="broken(wat)"/></class></api>','37.2')['records']
  self.assertEqual(rows[-1]['kind'],'unsupported');self.assertIn('descriptor',rows[-1]['extras']['parse_error'])

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tests/test_sdk_zip_metadata.py ===
import unittest,io,zipfile,pathlib,sys
sys.path.insert(0,str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'tooling'))
try:import sdk_zip_metadata as m
except ImportError:m=None
class MetadataRangeTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_only_selected_metadata_payload_is_read(self):
  self.assertIsNotNone(m)
  b=io.BytesIO()
  with zipfile.ZipFile(b,'w',zipfile.ZIP_STORED) as z:z.writestr('android.jar',b'UNSELECTED_BINARY'*10000);z.writestr('data/api-versions.xml','<api version="3"/>');z.writestr('source.properties','AndroidVersion.ApiLevel=37\nAndroidVersion.ApiMinor=2\n')
  raw=b.getvalue();calls=[]
  def get(start,count):calls.append((start,count));return raw[start:start+count]
  files,entries=m.extract_metadata(len(raw),get)
  self.assertEqual(files['data/api-versions.xml'],b'<api version="3"/>');self.assertNotIn('android.jar',files)
  self.assertLess(sum(n for _,n in calls),10000)
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tests/test_source_annotation_targets.py ===
import unittest,pathlib,tempfile,hashlib,json,gzip,zipfile,sqlite3,sys
sys.path.insert(0,str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'tooling'))
import build_catalog as b,sdk_api_index
import test_build as base_module
class SourceTargets(unittest.TestCase):
 fixture=base_module.BuildTests.fixture
 def source(self,r,spec,kind,name,data,**extra):
  (r/name).write_bytes(data);s=dict(spec['sources'][0],kind=kind,path=name,local_path=name,url='https://dl.google.com/fixture/'+name,sha256=hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),git_blob=hashlib.sha1(b'blob '+str(len(data)).encode()+b'\0'+data).hexdigest(),size_bytes=len(data));s.update(extra);spec['sources'].append(s);return s
 def annotation(self,r,spec,**extra):
  p=r/'ann.zip'
  with zipfile.ZipFile(p,'w') as z:z.writestr('a.xml','<root><item name="a.A void go(java.lang.String)"><annotation name="android.annotation.RequiresPermission"><val name="value" val="&quot;android.permission.CAMERA&quot;"/></annotation></item></root>')
  self.source(r,spec,'sdk-annotations','ann.zip',p.read_bytes(),**extra)
 def test_shadowed_type_variable_does_not_match_java_lang_alias(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   r=pathlib.Path(td);inv=self.fixture(r);spec=json.loads(inv.read_text());s=spec['sources'][0];data=(r/'api.txt').read_bytes().replace(b'public class A {',b'public class A<String> {');(r/'api.txt').write_bytes(data);s.update(sha256=hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),git_blob=hashlib.sha1(b'blob '+str(len(data)).encode()+b'\0'+data).hexdigest(),size_bytes=len(data));self.annotation(r,spec);inv.write_text(json.dumps(spec));b.build(inv,r/'out')
   db=sqlite3.connect(r/'out/catalog.sqlite');self.addCleanup(db.close);self.assertIsNone(db.execute('SELECT binding_id FROM annotation_evidence').fetchone()[0])
 def test_current_sdk_annotation_maps_to_exact_jvm_target(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   r=pathlib.Path(td);inv=self.fixture(r);spec=json.loads(inv.read_text());data=b'<api version="4"><class name="a/A"><method name="go(Ljava/lang/String;)V"/></class></api>';records=sdk_api_index.parse(data)['records'];cache=r/'index.gz'
   with gzip.open(cache,'wt') as f:
    for row in records:f.write(json.dumps(row)+'\n')
   self.source(r,spec,'sdk-api-index','index.xml',data,platform_api_version='37.2',metadata_cache=dict(path=cache.name,sha256=hashlib.sha256(cache.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),records=len(records)))
   self.annotation(r,spec,platform_api_version='37.2');inv.write_text(json.dumps(spec));b.build(inv,r/'out')
   db=sqlite3.connect(r/'out/catalog.sqlite');self.addCleanup(db.close);bid=db.execute('SELECT binding_id FROM annotation_evidence').fetchone()[0];self.assertEqual(bid,hashlib.sha256(b'jvm:a/A#go(Ljava/lang/String;)V').hexdigest())

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tests/test_source_integration.py ===
import unittest,pathlib,tempfile,json,hashlib,sqlite3,sys,zipfile,gzip
sys.path.insert(0,str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'tooling'))
import build_catalog as b
import test_build as base_module
class SourceIntegrationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    fixture=base_module.BuildTests.fixture

    def test_structured_aidl_members_survive_publication(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
            r=pathlib.Path(td);inv=self.fixture(r);spec=json.loads(inv.read_text())
            source=spec['sources'][2];data=b'package a; parcelable P { @nullable String name; int[] values={1,2}; }'
            (r/'IFoo.aidl').write_bytes(data)
            source.update(sha256=hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),git_blob=hashlib.sha1(b'blob '+str(len(data)).encode()+b'\x00'+data).hexdigest(),size_bytes=len(data))
            inv.write_text(json.dumps(spec));b.build(inv,r/'out')
            docs=[json.loads(line) for line in (r/'out/entries.jsonl').read_text().splitlines()]
            fields=[doc for doc in docs if doc['canonical_signature'] in ('aidl:a.P#name','aidl:a.P#values')]
            self.assertEqual(len(fields),2)
            self.assertTrue(all(doc['extensions']['wire_tag'] is None for doc in fields))

    def test_cached_java_and_current_index_import_with_distinct_evidence(self):
        import java_source, sdk_api_index
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
            r = pathlib.Path(td)
            inv = self.fixture(r)
            spec = json.loads(inv.read_text())
            for kind, name, data, parser in [('java-source', 'C.java', b'package a; public class C { @android.annotation.RequiresPermission("android.permission.CAMERA") public void f(int x){} }', lambda b: java_source.parse(b)['records']), ('sdk-api-index', 'api.xml', b'<api version="4"><class name="a/C"><method name="f(I)V"/><field name="VALUE"/></class></api>', lambda b: sdk_api_index.parse(b)['records'])]:
                (r / name).write_bytes(data)
                cache = r / (name + '.gz')
                records = parser(data)
                with gzip.open(cache, 'wt') as f:
                    for row in records:
                        f.write(json.dumps(row) + '\n')
                source = dict(spec['sources'][0], kind=kind, path=name, local_path=name, sha256=hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), git_blob=hashlib.sha1(b'blob ' + str(len(data)).encode() + b'\x00' + data).hexdigest(), size_bytes=len(data), url='https://dl.google.com/mock/' + name, metadata_cache=dict(path=cache.name, sha256=hashlib.sha256(cache.read_bytes()).hexdigest(), records=len(records)))
                spec['sources'].append(source)
            inv.write_text(json.dumps(spec))
            v = b.build(inv, r / 'out')
            self.assertEqual(v['counts']['sources'], 5)
            docs = [json.loads(l) for l in (r / 'out/entries.jsonl').read_text().splitlines()]
            self.assertTrue(any((d['canonical_signature'] == 'jvm:a/C#f(I)V' for d in docs)))
            java = next((d for d in docs if d['canonical_signature'] == 'java-source:a.C#f(int)'))
            self.assertEqual(java['permission']['name'], 'android.permission.CAMERA')
            jvm = next((d for d in docs if d['canonical_signature'] == 'jvm:a/C#f(I)V'))
            self.assertEqual(jvm['permission']['name'], 'android.permission.CAMERA')
            self.assertEqual(java['extensions']['proven_current_sdk_jvm_identity'], 'a/C#f(I)V')

    def test_cache_hash_failure_does_not_publish(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
            r = pathlib.Path(td)
            inv = self.fixture(r)
            spec = json.loads(inv.read_text())
            s = spec['sources'][0]
            s['kind'] = 'java-source'
            s['metadata_cache'] = dict(path='bad.gz', sha256='0' * 64, records=0)
            (r / 'bad.gz').write_bytes(b'broken')
            inv.write_text(json.dumps(spec))
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'Metadata cache SHA256 mismatch'):
                b.build(inv, r / 'out')
            self.assertFalse((r / 'out/catalog.sqlite').exists())

    def test_malformed_aidl_is_preserved_as_unknown(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
            r = pathlib.Path(td)
            inv = self.fixture(r)
            spec = json.loads(inv.read_text())
            s = spec['sources'][2]
            data = b'package a; @Broken( interface IFoo {'
            (r / 'IFoo.aidl').write_bytes(data)
            s.update(sha256=hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), git_blob=hashlib.sha1(b'blob ' + str(len(data)).encode() + b'\x00' + data).hexdigest(), size_bytes=len(data))
            inv.write_text(json.dumps(spec))
            b.build(inv, r / 'out')
            db = sqlite3.connect(r / 'out/catalog.sqlite')
            row = db.execute('SELECT raw_schema,parse_status FROM aidl_schemas').fetchone()
            self.assertEqual(row, (data.decode(), 'unsupported'))
            self.assertGreater(db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM issues').fetchone()[0], 0)
            db.close()

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/migrations/001-initial.sql ===
PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;
PRAGMA user_version=1;
CREATE TABLE catalog_meta(key TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,value TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE sources(source_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, path TEXT NOT NULL, url TEXT NOT NULL, revision TEXT NOT NULL, retrieved_at TEXT NOT NULL, sha256 TEXT NOT NULL, api_level INTEGER, channel TEXT NOT NULL, kind TEXT NOT NULL, license TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE bindings(binding_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, signature TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, package TEXT NOT NULL, owner TEXT, kind TEXT NOT NULL, name TEXT NOT NULL, return_type TEXT, arguments_json TEXT, raw_signature TEXT NOT NULL, codec_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'unknown');
CREATE INDEX binding_lookup ON bindings(package,owner,name,binding_id);
CREATE TABLE occurrences(binding_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES bindings, source_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sources, line INTEGER NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(binding_id,source_id,line));
CREATE TABLE binding_declarations(binding_id TEXT NOT NULL,source_id TEXT NOT NULL,line INTEGER NOT NULL,document_json TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(binding_id,source_id,line),FOREIGN KEY(binding_id,source_id,line) REFERENCES occurrences);
CREATE TABLE permission_definitions(name TEXT NOT NULL, source_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sources, protection_level TEXT, attributes_json TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(name,source_id));
CREATE TABLE permission_relations(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,binding_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES bindings,source_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sources,line INTEGER NOT NULL,annotation TEXT,logic TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(logic IN ('all','any','permission','access','conditional','manual','explicit-none','unknown')),expression_json TEXT NOT NULL,raw_annotation TEXT);
CREATE INDEX permission_lookup ON permission_relations(binding_id);
CREATE TABLE aidl_schemas(source_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES sources, package TEXT, descriptor TEXT, declaration_kind TEXT, raw_schema TEXT NOT NULL, parse_status TEXT NOT NULL, transaction_metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL, codec_status TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE issues(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, source_id TEXT REFERENCES sources,line INTEGER,category TEXT NOT NULL,reason TEXT NOT NULL,raw TEXT);
-- Contract 1.0.0: source records are append-only. Imports run in one transaction.
CREATE TRIGGER sources_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON sources BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported sources are immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER sources_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON sources BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported sources are immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER bindings_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON bindings BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported bindings are immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER bindings_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON bindings BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Suppress imported records using a local overlay'); END;
CREATE TABLE registries(registry_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, registry_version INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE CHECK(registry_version>=1), schema_version TEXT NOT NULL, android_version TEXT NOT NULL, api_level INTEGER, source_set_hash TEXT NOT NULL, created_at TEXT NOT NULL, state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('staged','validated','rejected','published','retired')), validation_json TEXT NOT NULL,UNIQUE(registry_id,state));
CREATE TABLE registry_sources(registry_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES registries,source_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sources,PRIMARY KEY(registry_id,source_id));
CREATE TABLE active_registry(singleton INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(singleton=1),registry_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES registries,published_state TEXT GENERATED ALWAYS AS ('published') VIRTUAL,FOREIGN KEY(registry_id,published_state) REFERENCES registries(registry_id,state) DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED);
CREATE TABLE overlays(entry_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, imported_binding_id TEXT REFERENCES bindings, canonical_signature TEXT NOT NULL, revision INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(revision>0), base_registry_id TEXT REFERENCES registries, base_record_hash TEXT, state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('active','suppressed','conflict','deleted')), document_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(document_json)), created_at TEXT NOT NULL, updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,FOREIGN KEY(entry_id,revision) REFERENCES entry_history(entry_id,revision) DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED,CHECK(imported_binding_id IS NULL OR entry_id=imported_binding_id));
CREATE INDEX overlay_lookup ON overlays(imported_binding_id,state);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX overlay_one_import ON overlays(imported_binding_id) WHERE imported_binding_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE TABLE entry_history(entry_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES overlays, revision INTEGER NOT NULL, operation TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(operation IN ('create','update','suppress','delete','restore','rebase','conflict')), document_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(document_json)), actor TEXT NOT NULL, occurred_at TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(entry_id,revision));
CREATE TRIGGER history_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON entry_history BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'History is append-only'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER history_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON entry_history BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'History is append-only'); END;
CREATE TABLE legacy_import_jobs(idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,request_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,registry_id TEXT REFERENCES registries,state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('staged','validated','rejected','published','retired')),result_json TEXT NOT NULL);
-- Bounded effective-view readers select current registry then join occurrences,
-- registry_sources and overlays. They MUST exclude suppressed/deleted/conflict
-- rows for invocation discovery and reject unknown semantic extensions.

CREATE TRIGGER occurrences_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON occurrences BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER occurrences_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON occurrences BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER binding_declarations_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON binding_declarations BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER binding_declarations_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON binding_declarations BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER permission_definitions_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON permission_definitions BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER permission_definitions_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON permission_definitions BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER permission_relations_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON permission_relations BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER permission_relations_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON permission_relations BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER aidl_schemas_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON aidl_schemas BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER aidl_schemas_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON aidl_schemas BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER issues_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON issues BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER issues_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON issues BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER registry_sources_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON registry_sources BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER registry_sources_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON registry_sources BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER registry_state_transition BEFORE UPDATE OF state ON registries
 WHEN new.state<>old.state AND NOT ((old.state='staged' AND new.state IN ('validated','rejected')) OR (old.state='validated' AND new.state='published') OR (old.state='published' AND new.state='retired'))
 BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Invalid registry state transition'); END;

CREATE TABLE idempotency_records(principal TEXT NOT NULL,operation TEXT NOT NULL,idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL,request_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(length(request_sha256)=64),response_status INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(response_status BETWEEN 100 AND 599),response_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(response_json)),resource_id TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(principal,operation,idempotency_key));
CREATE TABLE registry_events(event_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,actor TEXT NOT NULL,request_id TEXT NOT NULL,registry_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES registries,kind TEXT NOT NULL,occurred_at TEXT NOT NULL,result_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(result_json)));
CREATE TABLE registry_outbox(event_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES registry_events,affected_entry_ids_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(affected_entry_ids_json)),delivered_at TEXT);
CREATE TRIGGER registry_events_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON registry_events BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Registry events are append-only'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER registry_events_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON registry_events BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Registry events are append-only'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER overlay_revision_update BEFORE UPDATE ON overlays WHEN new.revision<=old.revision BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Every overlay write increments revision'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER overlay_canonical_insert BEFORE INSERT ON overlays WHEN new.imported_binding_id IS NOT NULL AND new.canonical_signature<>(SELECT signature FROM bindings WHERE binding_id=new.imported_binding_id) BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported identity/signature mismatch'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER overlay_canonical_update BEFORE UPDATE ON overlays WHEN new.entry_id<>old.entry_id OR new.canonical_signature<>old.canonical_signature OR (new.imported_binding_id IS NOT NULL AND new.canonical_signature<>(SELECT signature FROM bindings WHERE binding_id=new.imported_binding_id)) BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Canonical identity cannot change'); END;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX registries_one_published ON registries(state) WHERE state='published';
CREATE TRIGGER active_must_be_published_insert BEFORE INSERT ON active_registry WHEN (SELECT state FROM registries WHERE registry_id=new.registry_id) IS NOT 'published' BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Active registry must be published'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER active_must_be_published_update BEFORE UPDATE OF registry_id ON active_registry WHEN (SELECT state FROM registries WHERE registry_id=new.registry_id) IS NOT 'published' OR (new.registry_id<>old.registry_id AND (SELECT registry_version FROM registries WHERE registry_id=new.registry_id)<=(SELECT registry_version FROM registries WHERE registry_id=old.registry_id)) BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Publication requires a newer published registry'); END;

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/migrations/002-evidence-and-publication.sql ===
-- Additive migration from version 1. Entry/API JSON contracts retain version 1.0.0;
-- additions live under their explicitly reserved extensions/x-* namespaces.
PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;
BEGIN IMMEDIATE;
CREATE TABLE source_details(source_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES sources, document_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(document_json)));
CREATE TABLE parse_reports(source_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES sources, document_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(document_json)));
ALTER TABLE binding_declarations ADD COLUMN record_sha256 TEXT;
CREATE TABLE annotation_evidence(source_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sources, locator TEXT NOT NULL, binding_id TEXT REFERENCES bindings, scope TEXT NOT NULL, document_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(document_json)), PRIMARY KEY(source_id,locator));
CREATE INDEX annotation_target ON annotation_evidence(binding_id);
CREATE TABLE permission_symbols(symbol TEXT NOT NULL, value TEXT NOT NULL, source_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sources, line INTEGER NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(symbol,source_id,line));
CREATE TABLE registry_entries(registry_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES registries,entry_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES bindings(binding_id),document_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(document_json)),record_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(registry_id,entry_id));
CREATE TRIGGER source_details_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON source_details BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER source_details_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON source_details BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER annotation_evidence_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON annotation_evidence BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER annotation_evidence_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON annotation_evidence BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER permission_symbols_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON permission_symbols BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER permission_symbols_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON permission_symbols BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER registry_entries_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON registry_entries BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Publish a new registry instead'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER registry_entries_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON registry_entries BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Publish a new registry instead'); END;
-- ETag composition is identical for base, overlay, tombstone and history CAS.
CREATE TABLE catalog_view(singleton INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(singleton=1),registry_generation INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(registry_generation>0),overlay_generation INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(overlay_generation>=0));
INSERT INTO catalog_view VALUES(1,1,0);
CREATE TRIGGER overlay_generation_insert AFTER INSERT ON overlays BEGIN UPDATE catalog_view SET overlay_generation=overlay_generation+1; END;
CREATE TRIGGER overlay_generation_update AFTER UPDATE ON overlays BEGIN UPDATE catalog_view SET overlay_generation=overlay_generation+1; END;
CREATE TRIGGER overlay_generation_delete AFTER DELETE ON overlays BEGIN UPDATE catalog_view SET overlay_generation=overlay_generation+1; END;
CREATE TRIGGER registry_generation_switch AFTER UPDATE OF registry_id ON active_registry WHEN old.registry_id<>new.registry_id BEGIN UPDATE catalog_view SET registry_generation=max(registry_generation+1,(SELECT registry_version FROM registries WHERE registry_id=new.registry_id)); END;
CREATE TRIGGER parse_reports_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON parse_reports BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported reports are immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER parse_reports_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON parse_reports BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported reports are immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER registry_generation_initial AFTER INSERT ON active_registry BEGIN UPDATE catalog_view SET registry_generation=max(registry_generation,(SELECT registry_version FROM registries WHERE registry_id=new.registry_id)); END;
CREATE VIEW effective_entries AS
 SELECT e.entry_id,e.document_json,e.record_sha256,
 '"cat1:'||v.registry_generation||':'||e.record_sha256||':'||e.entry_id||':0:active"' AS etag
 FROM registry_entries e JOIN active_registry a ON a.registry_id=e.registry_id CROSS JOIN catalog_view v;
CREATE VIEW runtime_catalog AS
 SELECT e.entry_id,coalesce(o.document_json,e.document_json) AS document_json,
 '"cat1:'||v.registry_generation||':'||e.record_sha256||':'||e.entry_id||':'||coalesce(o.revision,0)||':'||coalesce(o.state,'active')||'"' AS etag
 FROM effective_entries e CROSS JOIN catalog_view v LEFT JOIN overlays o ON o.entry_id=e.entry_id
 WHERE o.entry_id IS NULL OR o.state='active'
 UNION ALL
 SELECT o.entry_id,o.document_json,'"cat1:'||v.registry_generation||':local:'||o.entry_id||':'||o.revision||':'||o.state||'"'
 FROM overlays o CROSS JOIN catalog_view v WHERE o.imported_binding_id IS NULL AND o.state='active' AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM effective_entries e WHERE e.entry_id=o.entry_id);
CREATE TRIGGER active_local_collision_insert BEFORE INSERT ON active_registry WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM overlays o JOIN registry_entries e ON e.entry_id=o.entry_id AND e.registry_id=new.registry_id WHERE o.imported_binding_id IS NULL AND o.state='active') BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Resolve local/import collision before publication'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER active_local_collision_update BEFORE UPDATE OF registry_id ON active_registry WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM overlays o JOIN registry_entries e ON e.entry_id=o.entry_id AND e.registry_id=new.registry_id WHERE o.imported_binding_id IS NULL AND o.state='active') BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Resolve local/import collision before publication'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER local_collision_insert BEFORE INSERT ON overlays WHEN new.imported_binding_id IS NULL AND new.state='active' AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM effective_entries e WHERE e.entry_id=new.entry_id) BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Use an imported overlay for an active imported identity'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER local_collision_update BEFORE UPDATE ON overlays WHEN new.imported_binding_id IS NULL AND new.state='active' AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM effective_entries e WHERE e.entry_id=new.entry_id) BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Use an imported overlay for an active imported identity'); END;
PRAGMA user_version=3;
COMMIT;

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT evidence/original-002-evidence-and-publication.sql ===
-- Additive migration from version 1. Entry/API JSON contracts retain version 1.0.0;
-- additions live under their explicitly reserved extensions/x-* namespaces.
PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;
BEGIN IMMEDIATE;
CREATE TABLE source_details(source_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES sources, document_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(document_json)));
CREATE TABLE parse_reports(source_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES sources, document_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(document_json)));
ALTER TABLE binding_declarations ADD COLUMN record_sha256 TEXT;
CREATE TABLE annotation_evidence(source_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sources, locator TEXT NOT NULL, binding_id TEXT REFERENCES bindings, scope TEXT NOT NULL, document_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(document_json)), PRIMARY KEY(source_id,locator));
CREATE INDEX annotation_target ON annotation_evidence(binding_id);
CREATE TABLE permission_symbols(symbol TEXT NOT NULL, value TEXT NOT NULL, source_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sources, line INTEGER NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(symbol,source_id,line));
CREATE TABLE effective_entries(entry_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES bindings(binding_id), document_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(document_json)), record_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL, etag TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TRIGGER source_details_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON source_details BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER source_details_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON source_details BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER annotation_evidence_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON annotation_evidence BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER annotation_evidence_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON annotation_evidence BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER permission_symbols_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON permission_symbols BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER permission_symbols_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON permission_symbols BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER effective_entries_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON effective_entries BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Publish a new registry instead'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER effective_entries_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON effective_entries BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Publish a new registry instead'); END;
CREATE VIEW runtime_catalog AS
 SELECT e.entry_id,coalesce(o.document_json,e.document_json) AS document_json,
 CASE WHEN o.entry_id IS NULL THEN e.etag ELSE '"overlay:'||o.entry_id||':'||o.revision||'"' END AS etag
 FROM effective_entries e LEFT JOIN overlays o ON o.imported_binding_id=e.entry_id
 WHERE o.entry_id IS NULL OR o.state='active'
 UNION ALL
 SELECT o.entry_id,o.document_json,'"overlay:'||o.entry_id||':'||o.revision||'"'
 FROM overlays o WHERE o.imported_binding_id IS NULL AND o.state='active';
PRAGMA user_version=2;
COMMIT;

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT design/sources/architecture.mmd ===
flowchart TD
    Client[Agent or ADB-forwarded host] --> Server[Persistent MCP service]
    Server --> Gate[Shared client policy gate]
    Sources[SDK AIDL and protocol metadata] --> Registry[Full binding registry]
    Registry --> Gate
    Owner[Local owner UI] --> Gate
    Gate --> Binding[Generated or generic binding]
    Gate --> ADB[Paired ADB screen and tap]
    Gate --> Runtime[Optional Avalonia runtime]
    Gate --> Data[SQLite audit and configuration]
    Data --> Logs[Per-client optional logs]

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT design/sources/request-sequence.mmd ===
sequenceDiagram
    participant C as Client
    participant P as Policy
    participant U as Owner
    participant G as Gate
    participant A as Adapter
    C->>P: Authenticated call
    P->>P: Validate plan
    opt Ask configured
        P->>U: Bound request
        U->>P: Decision
        break Denied or expired
            P-->>C: No dispatch
        end
    end
    P->>G: Plan and revisions
    G->>G: Recheck and audit
    G->>A: Typed operation
    A-->>P: Result or unknown
    P->>P: Filter and bounded log
    P-->>C: Response
    Note over G,A: No replay of uncertain taps

=== END ARTIFACT ===
